[zircon_runtime/99zi-runtime-preference-settings-scope-storage-overlay-bounded-io-generation-fence-durability-migration-multi-process-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/99zi-runtime-preference-settings-scope-storage-overlay-bounded-io-generation-fence-durability-migration-multi-process-product-integration-current-source-review.md)完成Runtime Preference、Settings、Scope、Storage、Overlay、Bounded I/O、Durability、Migration、Multi-Process与产品闭环当前源码刷新：Runtime已有中立SPI、bounded read/admission、read-your-write overlay、same-key coalescing、fence、panic/pre-start deadline和atomic backend，Editor已有typed schema/layer/snapshot/versioned atomic write与真实退出fence；但HostProvided write/remove仍在flush前误报Durable，single-active lane与无界guard Drop让hung backend冻结全局并阻止退出，address/root无product/principal隔离，record无revision/CAS/watch。WOC三个Stored类型无生产构造，refresh/harvest只有测试caller，Inventory effect无consumer；Editor仍是第二filesystem authority，User设置没有通用save owner。5项P0全Open，58项P1为47 Open、11 Partial，14项P2为13 Open、1 Partial；40项Gate为32 Fail、8 Partial。本文冻结100个Zircon selected文件和20个Unreal/Godot/Fyrox/Bevy/Unity Graphics参考文件，目标是`App identity + Runtime PreferenceService + schema/namespace catalog + fair scheduler + revisioned provider + durability receipt + Editor/WOC hard cutover`。

Runtime UI Template/Component/Binding专项冻结290个production/cross-product Rust文件、50,717行、1,742,740 bytes，16个聚焦测试文件、5,730行，以及Editor/WOC 267份产品ZUI、48,298行。当前target assignment可验证/编译/打包却无runtime executor，`ParamRef`合法穿过compiler后在Runtime与Editor均返回`None`，component event靠CamelCase名称子串猜测而产品使用lower_snake route，重复component instance又通过全surface最小node id串读，hot reload只回报target/dirty而不重建、迁移或重绑。报告登记5项P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是typed schema/endpoint/event/action、compiled binding artifact、instance scope、batch mutation transaction、model subscription、command admission和generation-qualified reload。详见[zircon_runtime/74-runtime-ui-template-component-binding-expression-model-event-command-hot-reload-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/74-runtime-ui-template-component-binding-expression-model-event-command-hot-reload-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime UI Component Catalog/Widget Behavior专项冻结447个production/cross-product Rust文件、61,461行、2,218,453 bytes，69个聚焦测试文件、17,374行，以及Editor/WOC 282份产品ZUI。当前showcase 69项、Material 211项只重合22项，v2绕过schema/capability/fallback并接受任意非空native id，20余个reducer只接showcase demo，live surface另走字符串behavior/mutation路径，输入、渲染和a11y又维护不同classifier。报告登记6项P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是单一component authority、typed implementation/facet、schema-safe state transaction、controlled proposal/commit、component-specific render/a11y和产品conformance。详见[zircon_runtime/75-runtime-ui-component-catalog-widget-behavior-state-reducer-interaction-semantics-accessibility-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/75-runtime-ui-component-catalog-widget-behavior-state-reducer-interaction-semantics-accessibility-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime UI Layout/Measure/Arrange专项冻结107个production/cross-product Rust文件、20,378行、702,381 bytes，54个聚焦测试文件、13,783行，以及48份真实Editor/WOC ZUI。当前public `UiLayoutStyle`与retained tree断链，Taffy每个container每次pass只临时建立parent+leaf浅树；`.zui` track/span可直接驱动无界分配，backend fallback没有完整复核request，所谓virtualization仍全树materialize/measure后只隐藏frame，dynamic product又把physical viewport直接当logical root size。报告登记3项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是bounded compiled layout artifact、persistent backend graph、constraint-aware intrinsic measure、真实data virtualization、logical/physical geometry barrier和产品scale receipt。详见[zircon_runtime/76-runtime-ui-layout-box-model-measure-arrange-flex-grid-overflow-scroll-virtualization-dpi-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/76-runtime-ui-layout-box-model-measure-arrange-flex-grid-overflow-scroll-virtualization-dpi-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime UI Input/Dispatch/Routing专项冻结182个production/cross-product Rust文件、26,075行、892,689 bytes与96个聚焦测试文件、30,613行、460项测试。当前typed event/effect/result、preview/bubble、focus restore、navigation、capture、drag、IME context和AccessKit转换是真实底座；但`apply_dispatch_reply_core`顺序直写effects，尾项失败时不回滚已提交的focus/capture/drag/component前缀，Dynamic Runtime、Editor和WOC又各自转换与分派输入。报告登记1项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是generation-qualified input envelope、单一window/router、原子effect transaction、per-seat focus/capture/gesture、async host result、window teardown和统一产品receipt；Runtime11A/11B/43/56/75/76与Editor01继续拥有既有父问题。详见[zircon_runtime/77-runtime-ui-input-dispatch-routing-focus-navigation-pointer-capture-gesture-drag-drop-ime-window-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/77-runtime-ui-input-dispatch-routing-focus-navigation-pointer-capture-gesture-drag-drop-ime-window-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime UI Accessibility专项冻结392个production/cross-product文件、69,876行、3,698,668 bytes，21个聚焦测试文件、8,387行、123项测试；参考冻结25个文件、19,473行。当前中立DTO、确定性extract、名称/关系诊断、action模块和AccessKit codec是真实底座，但317份tracked ZUI、5,594个node section没有任何显式a11y/relation authoring，snapshot/action缺publication generation，relation/state/text/range/live schema不足，converter存在字段丢失和必拒绝动作映射，App/Editor/WOC没有真实per-window screen reader adapter。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是typed semantic source、compiled relation graph、incremental publication、platform adapter、atomic action receipt、live event与真实AT资格；Runtime11A/11B/43/75/76/77和Editor23继续拥有既有父问题。详见[zircon_runtime/78-runtime-ui-accessibility-semantic-tree-name-description-relation-state-action-live-region-platform-adapter-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/78-runtime-ui-accessibility-semantic-tree-name-description-relation-state-action-live-region-platform-adapter-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime UI Renderer当前源码专项冻结264个聚焦文件、65,322行、2,248,869 bytes与544项test attribute，其中production为200个文件、46,288行；参考冻结23个文件、24,534行。真实WGPU shape/image/text、bitmap/SDF atlas、dirty upload、dependency batching、bounded image cache、retained surface与GPU stats可保留，native image admission也已正确执行一次alpha预乘；但Runtime11C三项P0仍全部开放，且`UiBatchPlan`/render transform/draw effect无产品consumer、普通Editor full/damage frame保持`generation=None`、runtime icon 4096 clamp后可继续生成越界slot/UV、native surface只接受Win32与non-sRGB UNORM。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是唯一ordered `UiPresentationArtifact`、clip/layer/composite graph、共享device resource service、damage-correct presenter、统一color/receipt和同负载性能资格；三项继承P0继续由Runtime11C唯一计数。详见[zircon_runtime/79-runtime-ui-renderer-display-list-paint-order-clip-transform-opacity-atlas-text-glyph-batch-wgpu-submit-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/79-runtime-ui-renderer-display-list-paint-order-clip-transform-opacity-atlas-text-glyph-batch-wgpu-submit-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Font Foundation当前源码专项冻结96个聚焦文件、22,207行、852,987 bytes与229项test attribute，其中production为68个文件、15,789行；另冻结103,624-byte默认TTC。当前face metadata、bounded fallback/effective-instance cache、variation canonicalization、batch handle registry、color glyph storage和SDF source/offline budget是真实底座；但artifact仍没有raw font bytes，runtime必须重开source path，process-global DB/service/registry与默认system discovery不能隔离project/session，默认collection又依赖主机family。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是`FontBlobArtifact + FontCollectionSnapshot + FontCollectionService + ResolvedFontFaceLease + ResolvedGlyphStatus`；cook断链与synthetic rasterizable glyph两项继承P0继续由Runtime11B唯一计数。详见[zircon_runtime/80-runtime-font-asset-source-cook-database-face-fallback-variation-color-resolved-glyph-cache-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/80-runtime-font-asset-source-cook-database-face-fallback-variation-color-resolved-glyph-cache-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Text Shaping / Unicode / BiDi / Script Run / Cluster / Line Break / Wrap / Layout专项冻结162个Rust文件、36,821行、1,227,674 bytes与493项test attribute，其中production为114个文件、25,861行、859,391 bytes；冻结时15个范围内路径dirty。当前RustyBuzz横排/竖排、`unicode-bidi`、UAX14、grapheme、hard-line streaming和resolved glyph artifact是真实底座；但normalization被禁用，BCP-47、ScriptExtensions、emoji、locale tailoring、dictionary/hyphenation、cluster caret与document增量合同不足，UI/interface还会再次猜测visual order和synthetic glyph。新增1项P0来自删除`TEXT_SHAPING_RUN_MAX_BYTES`定义及re-export后7个测试文件24处引用仍未迁移；禁止恢复会改变logical line语义的旧cap。报告另登记48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是`UnicodeDataSnapshot + TextAnalysisArtifact + ShapingOutcome + GlyphClusterMap + ParagraphLayoutArtifact + DocumentLayoutSession`；Runtime11B synthetic glyph P0继续由原报告唯一计数。详见[zircon_runtime/81-runtime-text-shaping-unicode-bidi-script-run-cluster-line-break-wrap-layout-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/81-runtime-text-shaping-unicode-bidi-script-run-cluster-line-break-wrap-layout-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Text Editing / Document / Selection / Caret / Hit Test / IME Composition / Clipboard / Secure Text专项冻结117个文件、22,112行、747,860 bytes与183项test attribute，其中production为96个文件、15,713行、521,889 bytes；冻结时0个范围内路径dirty。当前grapheme insert/delete、resolved-layout hit-test、bounded surrounding text和winit/App IME接线是真实底座；但widget属性与component reducer形成双文档authority，没有document revision/transaction/history，visual caret/navigation不闭合，preedit混入committed `String`，composition rect/clause在host/ABI链收缩，clipboard request又没有App consumer或result。WOC密码字段使用`input_kind=password`却不触发secure classifier，明文进入render、snapshot、event/binding和accessibility；该P0继续由Runtime11B唯一计数，Runtime77继续拥有通用effect非原子P0。本报告新增0项P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是`TextDocumentSnapshot + TextEditTransaction + TextSelectionSet + TextCompositionSession + TextInputHostSession + SecureTextPolicy + ClipboardHostResult`。详见[zircon_runtime/82-runtime-text-editing-document-selection-caret-hit-test-ime-composition-clipboard-secure-text-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/82-runtime-text-editing-document-selection-caret-hit-test-ime-composition-clipboard-secure-text-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime RHI / WGPU Adapter / Device / Capability / Resource / Command / Queue / Submission / Completion / Readback / Surface / Device Loss当前源码专项冻结`zr_rhi` production/tests 15个文件、4,800行，`zr_rhi_wgpu` production-path/tests 61个文件、19,244行，产品集成34个文件、12,500行；参考冻结五引擎19个文件、28,985行。中立descriptor/validation、WGPU capability映射、三槽异步readback、GPU timer/statistics和shared-device UI composition是真实底座；但中立`RenderDevice`没有production实现且非object-safe，唯一实现被`#[cfg(test)]`隔离，capability声明的queue/async/indirect/multi-draw/capture又无法由中立接口执行。产品没有DeviceGeneration、SubmissionTicket、targeted completion、deferred retirement或device-loss supervisor，readback缺同device证明/quota/exact terminal，Surface/UI仍可创建第二device并保留大量ad-hoc submit和整设备阻塞。报告新增3项P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是`RhiDeviceOwner + DeviceProfile + ResourceRegistry + SubmissionService + CompletionService + ReadbackService + SurfaceService + DeviceErrorSupervisor`；Runtime90接替09A的RHI currentness，Runtime89继续拥有Render Graph compiler/execution packet。详见[zircon_runtime/90-runtime-rhi-wgpu-adapter-device-capability-resource-command-queue-submission-completion-readback-surface-device-loss-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/90-runtime-rhi-wgpu-adapter-device-capability-resource-command-queue-submission-completion-readback-surface-device-loss-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Texture / Image / Cubemap / Array / Volume / Format / Sampler / Mip / Compression / Upload / Streaming / Residency / Budget / Eviction / Virtual Texture当前源码专项冻结Runtime production 99个文件、23,316行，focused tests 4个文件、1,375行，Texture importer production/tests 40个文件、9,683行，direct texture callsite 86个文件、27,520行；参考冻结五引擎26个文件、19,780行。DDS/KTX/KTX2/ASTC upload plan、KTX2 Zstd/Zlib展开、BC5 normal转码、Box/Kaiser mip kernel、artifact integrity和mip planner是真实底座；但whole bincode + generic 64 KiB chunk不能按mip/layer/page读取，submission同步load/decode/clone/create，首次full resident后同步replacement，compressed无partial residency，demand只含main-view material texture，普通D3与SVT/VT产品链缺失，86处texture create与83处write仍无唯一owner。本篇不重复新增P0，09D的6项继承P0仍开放；新增48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是统一Schema/Build/Artifact/Generation/Upload/Residency/Budget/Sampler/Virtual Texture owners。详见[zircon_runtime/92-runtime-texture-image-cubemap-array-volume-format-sampler-mip-compression-upload-streaming-residency-budget-eviction-virtual-texture-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/92-runtime-texture-image-cubemap-array-volume-format-sampler-mip-compression-upload-streaming-residency-budget-eviction-virtual-texture-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Mesh / Geometry / Section / LOD / Instancing / Skinning / Morph / Deformation / Bounds / Collision / Streaming当前源码专项冻结production/contract/consumer 420个Rust文件、80,945行、2,972,710 bytes，focused tests 37个文件、10,746行、108项test attribute；参考冻结五引擎35个文件、49,725行。typed mesh/model文档、section/LOD、artifact integrity、GPU mesh cache、局部morph changed-row upload与可见性/streaming接入点是真实底座；但高优先级glTF插件弱于builtin且产品测试绕开真实插件，Model/Mesh形成双份几何真相，GPU Scene每draw固定注册1实例并以单位球近似bounds，GPU skin提交前先做CPU skin且palette来自bind local而非inverse bind，collision则构造逐三角静态compound。本篇不重复新增P0，Runtime69的材质override持久化P0仍开放；新增48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，目标是统一Schema/Build/Artifact/Generation/Residency/Draw Packet/Deformation/Bounds/Collision Cook owners。详见[zircon_runtime/93-runtime-mesh-geometry-section-lod-instancing-skinning-morph-deformation-bounds-collision-streaming-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/93-runtime-mesh-geometry-section-lod-instancing-skinning-morph-deformation-bounds-collision-streaming-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

First-Party Sound专项冻结Sound全包1,307个文件、23,903行、907,000 bytes与362项test attribute。Kira playback、typed audio合同、图校验与局部空间/声学算法真实存在，但普通App和Editor Host均不装配Sound provider，runtime catalog只链接root而不链接两个optional feature，editor catalog对Sound为0。真实Kira路径绕过source_environment，effect/send与两个内建preset不可执行，三项send routing门仍为红色；33个Editor command无factory、五份ZUI主要为Space，optional runtime/editor/dist也只发布descriptor/capability。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立SoundActivationPlan、audio render/device/world/resource/mixer generation、真实spatial/acoustic/timeline、Editor document/audition和包装形态parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime08B、Editor17、Plugins01/06/07及既有failure owner负责。详见[zircon_plugins/11-first-party-sound-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-mixer-spatial-reverb-timeline-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/11-first-party-sound-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-mixer-spatial-reverb-timeline-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Physics专项冻结Physics全包93个文件、13,207行、462,852 bytes与82项test attribute。中立Physics合同、generation handle、命令预算、world replacement epoch、builtin参考后端、可选Jolt body lifecycle和ragdoll profile是真实基础，但普通App/runtime catalog/editor catalog均不装配Physics；generated source虽然显式链接provider，却没有Jolt feature，NativeDynamic则只投影stateless metadata。Jolt query方法为空、contact/trigger绕回近似pair scan、constraint是native step后的Rust projection，builtin没有碰撞响应却可Ready；四份Editor ZUI均为Space业务占位，overlay与ragdoll command只打开view。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立PhysicsActivationPlan、单一fixed clock、per-world slot、增量Scene bridge、native filter/query/event/constraint、collision artifact、真实Editor与载体parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime08A、Editor18、Plugins01/06和开放overlay failure负责。详见[zircon_plugins/12-first-party-physics-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-simulation-collision-joint-ragdoll-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/12-first-party-physics-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-simulation-collision-joint-ragdoll-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Animation专项冻结Animation主包170个文件、18,172行、634,036 bytes与129项test attribute。compiled clip evaluator、PosePool、graph/state program、layer/mask、IK、事件背压和replacement epoch是真实基础，但普通Client/Editor Host运行core fallback而不链接插件provider，两套同名manager/module可独立漂移，插件内部legacy与compiled evaluator也未收敛。首方glTF animation仍是placeholder，GPU palette没有renderer consumer；八份Editor ZUI缺失，Graph/Timeline operation无产品handler，NativeDynamic只是stateless metadata shell。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立AnimationActivationPlan、唯一provider/evaluator、typed prepared artifact、stable target/pose、明确phase、真实Editor与render/physics/carrier parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime08C、Editor14、Plugins01/06/07/08及三份开放failure负责。详见[zircon_plugins/13-first-party-animation-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-skeleton-clip-pose-graph-state-machine-ik-skinning-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/13-first-party-animation-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-skeleton-clip-pose-graph-state-machine-ik-skinning-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Navigation专项冻结三个canonical root共217个tracked文件、52,108行、1,671,981 bytes与169项test attribute，其中51个vendored Recast/Detour文件是真实算法底座。普通Client却运行Rust fallback，Editor Host运行source Recast，Server没有Navigation产品路径；raw triangle DTO/TOML会在每次native query重建`dtNavMesh`，TileCache只有单个硬编码layer，Bake使用quad/顶面/AABB等假几何且product operation明确拒绝Bake。任意obstacle/off-mesh link又会清空crowd转legacy，全World JSON扫描、直接Transform写回、11份ZUI占位和NativeDynamic metadata shell没有闭合。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立NavigationActivationPlan、真实geometry/cooked Detour tiles、per-world generation、persistent query/crowd/tile-cache、movement authority、真实Editor和carrier parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime08D、Editor19、Plugins01/06/07、Runtime42及五份开放failure负责。详见[zircon_plugins/14-first-party-navigation-source-native-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-recast-detour-crowd-tilecache-query-bake-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/14-first-party-navigation-source-native-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-recast-detour-crowd-tilecache-query-bake-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party AI专项冻结canonical AI 86个tracked文件、17,667行、609,467 bytes与109项test attribute，连同App、catalog、builtin row、Editor产品资源和Vampire示例共113个文件、23,528行、824,401 bytes与137项test attribute。dense tree、typed Blackboard、observer、owner-aware registry与perception组件是真实基础；普通Client/Editor/Server却没有一致provider，editor catalog对AI为0，NativeDynamic仅有metadata。Vampire唯一行为树被归为generic Data、schema与当前descriptor不兼容，场景只存字符串且脚本镜像决策，没有生产loader/compiler/registration；runtime又是global manager、递归无预算executor、全World感知扫描、缺Physics时Sight判可见并完全没有EQS。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立AiActivationPlan、真实source/compiler/artifact、per-world generation、budgeted scheduler、Perception/EQS、产品Editor和carrier parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime08F、Editor20、Plugins01/06/07、Runtime42等canonical owner负责。详见[zircon_plugins/15-first-party-ai-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-behavior-tree-blackboard-perception-eqs-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/15-first-party-ai-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-behavior-tree-blackboard-perception-eqs-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Zr VM Language专项冻结插件包40个tracked文件、4,573行、163,822 bytes与41项test attribute，连同Runtime VM、App/catalog、产品文档与Vampire共186个路径、31,006行文本、1,290,317 bytes与305项test attribute。具体backend owner、dense callsite、catalog generation guard、capability-gated extension、显式drop顺序、GC预算与hot-reload state migration是真实基础；普通Client/Editor Host却不链接provider，NativeDynamic dist也没有真实backend或执行面。真实VM依赖dirty外部仓库和本机DLL，所有实例共享进程mutex，value/reflection/state仍受窄标量与JSON限制；10个Vampire产品测试从Runtime移交后继续ignore。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立LanguageActivationPlan、hermetic toolchain/artifact、typed host transaction、per-world runtime、内存/GC、Editor/debug/profiling和产品资格；硬阻塞继续由Runtime07/21、Editor31、Plugins01/06、Interface04/05/07及四份开放failure拥有。详见[zircon_plugins/16-first-party-zr-vm-language-source-runtime-dist-catalog-reflection-callsite-host-interface-gc-hot-reload-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/16-first-party-zr-vm-language-source-runtime-dist-catalog-reflection-callsite-host-interface-gc-hot-reload-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Virtual Geometry专项冻结全包237个tracked文本文件、37,539行、1,390,191 bytes与214项test attribute。typed residency/slot、hierarchy/cluster DTO、CPU reference、GPU buffer/readback和Render Framework descriptor是真实底座；但五个公共executor只validate context后返回成功，私有GPU resources没有production构造点，两份WGSL不执行cluster cull/raster/material。Cook的`ZVG0`只存triangle range并回读完整原mesh，普通mesh shader又通过`joints.x/y`读取实验payload并静默fallback。默认profile/viewport/PBR viewer不启用，editor catalog无provider、`authoring.zui`不存在，NativeDynamic仅有metadata。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立自包含artifact、page store/persistent pool、GPU cull/raster/visibility/material产品链、真实Editor和carrier parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime09B/09C/09D、Runtime04、Editor22/32与Plugins01/04/06拥有。详见[zircon_plugins/17-first-party-virtual-geometry-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-asset-cook-cluster-page-streaming-culling-raster-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/17-first-party-virtual-geometry-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-asset-cook-cluster-page-streaming-culling-raster-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Texture专项冻结`texture`、主/旧importer、Runtime Texture/builtin ingest、catalog与App组装共113个文件、20,388行、699,980 bytes及257项test attribute。DDS/KTX/ASTC parser、BC5、typed upload readiness和RGBA32F IBL是可保留底座；但主importer的tracked mip kernel当前不可编译，HDR/EXR被量化为RGBA8，settings和测试允许format/payload矛盾，cube/array外部文件又旁路source broker。主importer未进provider catalog/profile，默认App也不启用提供`texture`的base feature，editor模板缺失，两个dist均为metadata shell。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立唯一source/recipe/canonical image/platform artifact/runtime generation/editor document/carrier/product composition链；五项硬阻塞继续由Editor35、Plugins07/08/06、Runtime04/09D与Plugins01拥有。详见[zircon_plugins/18-first-party-texture-source-importer-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-image-cubemap-array-volume-compression-streaming-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/18-first-party-texture-source-importer-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-image-cubemap-array-volume-compression-streaming-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Hybrid GI专项冻结全包270个tracked文件、43,848行、1,579,121 bytes及237项test attribute。typed provider、四段Render Graph、Mesh SDF/Global SDF page table、probe trace、temporal resolve与readback ring是真实基础；但产品仍使用固定`8 x 8` trace tile、每mesh一个球形card、16页Surface Cache、4个`4 x 4 x 4` voxel clipmap及32个`4 x 4` RGBA8 probe，Surface Cache没有捕获真实surface，collector又在全局mutex中clone/prepare/dispatch并以大量细碎readback形成队首阻塞。HRT执行、真实Editor authoring、profile truth和source/native parity均未闭合。报告登记0项新增P0、56项P1、14项P2与36项资格门；算法本体14项P0继续由Runtime09F3拥有，共享基础继续由Runtime09A-09F2/09H1/28、Editor22及Plugins01/04/06/08拥有。详见[zircon_plugins/19-first-party-hybrid-gi-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-scene-representation-surface-cache-global-sdf-radiance-cache-probe-trace-denoise-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/19-first-party-hybrid-gi-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-scene-representation-surface-cache-global-sdf-radiance-cache-probe-trace-denoise-product-integration-review.md)。

Plugin SDK Example、Native Dynamic Fixture与Editor Contribution Fixture专项冻结三组carrier、产品catalog/loader/manifest consumer及聚焦测试共47个文件、11,290行、421,220 bytes。当前Editor build按物理目录扫描所有顶层manifest，把fixture/sample生成builtin descriptor并推进Default phase；PackageKind又只能表达Standard/FeatureExtension，无法隔离Sample/TestFixture/DeveloperTool。SDK sample缺资源且source/file/native声明漂移，没有任何交付形态能兑现完整workflow；Native Fixture的`write:world` system与event没有可观察行为，可破坏性negative variant也没有独立artifact identity；Editor Fixture发布死command和纯metadata contribution，现有gate并不构建加载真实DLL。报告登记3项P0、40项P1、10项P2与32项资格门，目标是强类型PackageRole、显式ProductPluginCatalog、ArtifactEligibilityDecision、carrier parity receipt、negative artifact store与跨平台真实DLL lifecycle资格。详见[zircon_plugins/20-plugin-sdk-example-native-editor-fixture-test-carrier-artifact-isolation-product-truth-review.md](zircon_plugins/20-plugin-sdk-example-native-editor-fixture-test-carrier-artifact-isolation-product-truth-review.md)。

Runtime Picking专项冻结`core/framework/picking`及其测试29个文件、2,958行、90,963 bytes与22项test。RayMap、确定性hit排序、hover diff和局部事件状态机是真实底座；但完整pipeline/event/backend没有production caller，Editor只在手工构造`PointerHits`后消费route/debug，携带的`runtime_input`无人读取。同一pointer跨viewport会在hover/event层被合并，fixed `HitTarget`和raw `u64`无法表达代际身份，backend仍是同步、不可失败、ray-only合同；projection override、capture/gesture、失效/退役语义及renderer/physics/UI产品backend均未闭合。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与36项资格门；父问题继续由Editor03、Runtime12/23/24、Render04/09A与PERF-MVP-332拥有。详见[zircon_runtime/47-runtime-picking-pointer-ray-hit-hover-drag-drop-event-backend-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/47-runtime-picking-pointer-ray-hit-hover-drag-drop-event-backend-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime-wide State当前源码专项冻结19个focused文件、1,710行、55,638 bytes与12项test。typed current/next、identity suppression、state-pair哈希hook索引和锁外dispatch是真实底座；但排除测试/facade后product caller仍为0，transition只能手工apply且未进入Runtime03阶段表，单`NextState`静默覆盖、`insert_state`伪造`None -> new`、history无界全量clone、hook无token/generation/quiescence、panic截断fan-out、poison伪健康且TypeId/type_name不能承担schema/ABI。公开行为测试与Runtime48 truth freeze形成两项局部currentness进展，但inline/Runtime15源码形状guard和“poison-safe”声明仍stale。Runtime48账本为0项本地P0、46项P1 Open、2项Partial、12项P2 Open；36项Gate为33 Fail、1 Partial、2 Pass。父问题继续由Runtime01/02/03/05/07/22/24/38/41/46、Tooling35与PERF-MVP-320拥有。详见[zircon_runtime/99z-runtime-wide-state-next-state-transition-hook-history-schedule-scope-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/99z-runtime-wide-state-next-state-transition-hook-history-schedule-scope-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Camera当前源码专项冻结114个Zircon focused文件、23,687行、854,904 bytes与189项test declaration，并逐文件对照17个Unreal/Bevy/Godot/Fyrox/Unity Graphics参考文件。World v2已保存裸`active_camera`，render target generation、stack、view-family和多类history是真实底座；但active identity仍无World/source generation与prefab/cook合同，Scene source无法表达stack/clear-depth/独立layers/dynamic-resolution/projection override，`Perspective`仍兼任absence sentinel。dynamic Orbit、script和AI继续绕过Director直接读写global camera，focus loss与wheel语义错误未收敛，至少七类per-camera map也没有逐camera retirement。Runtime37的72项P1重判为65 Open、7 Partial，16项P2全Open；40项Gate为35 Fail、5 Partial。删除subtree可清空全部camera的父correctness阻断继续由既有World/Editor owner关闭。详见[zircon_runtime/99za-runtime-camera-endpoint-director-rig-controller-blend-shake-cut-history-multiview-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/99za-runtime-camera-endpoint-director-rig-controller-blend-shake-cut-history-multiview-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Gameplay Framework当前源码专项冻结153个Zircon focused文件、29,074行、1,094,769 bytes与281项test declaration，并逐文件对照25个Unreal/Bevy/Godot/Fyrox/Unity Graphics参考文件。Gameplay module capability ledger、dynamic session teardown、Level replacement epoch/currentness、Dynamic Scene transaction、UI-first input、typed Net DTO、scene ticket与WOC私有事务是可保留底座；但production没有GameInstance/WorldContext/Experience/GameRule/Player/Controller/Pawn/Possession/Travel owner，scene-transition只有单槽producer而无consumer，Level registry无unload/remove，Vampire和WOC继续旁路统一框架。Runtime38的72项P1重判为49 Open、23 Partial，16项P2全Open；40项Gate为30 Fail、9 Partial、1个文档静态Pass。性能优于Unreal仍无同场景/硬件/画质/网络raw receipt。详见[zircon_runtime/99zb-gameplay-framework-review.md](zircon_runtime/99zb-gameplay-framework-review.md)。

Runtime Debug Gizmo专项冻结18个focused文件、2,715行、82,535 bytes与12项test。九类typed command、稳定记录顺序、CPU shape tessellation与最终WGPU line提交是真实底座；但`GizmoBuffer`、`GizmoAsset`、`RetainedGizmo`和extract/append没有production caller，Editor、Navigation、AI与Virtual Geometry全部直写overlay DTO。group/width/depth bias/render layer/screen scale不进入渲染，retained丢失config且没有handle/generation/owner/TTL，Axis、Sphere、Circle与AABB transform在旋转或缩放下错误；最终renderer又逐帧创建buffer、逐icon提交且无view filter/budget/diagnostics。报告登记0项新增P0、56项P1、14项P2与36项资格门；PERF-MVP-333、Runtime09A/09B/23/24/47、Editor03、Runtime08D/08F与Plugins14/15继续拥有父问题。详见[zircon_runtime/49-runtime-debug-gizmo-command-buffer-retained-extract-filter-budget-render-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/49-runtime-debug-gizmo-command-buffer-retained-extract-filter-budget-render-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Manager Resolver专项冻结37个focused文件、7,351行、269,212 bytes与29项test。弱Core resolver、typed handle、generation复核及底层`ServiceCallGuard`是真实底座；但production manager facade解析后返回裸`Arc<T>`，调用不进入in-flight drain，原生trait object可能越过DLL卸载边界。raw concrete与wrapped trait两套manager并存，descriptor无contract/scope/provider/ABI/affinity，句柄index不直接寻址，Graphics、dynamic session、render loop和Editor viewport仍反复做名称hash、全局锁、downcast与Arc clone。报告登记0项新增P0、56项P1、14项P2与36项资格门；Runtime01/02/24/42/46、Interface01、Plugins01、App01与PERF-MVP-628继续拥有父问题。详见[zircon_runtime/50-runtime-manager-resolver-named-service-handle-generation-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/50-runtime-manager-resolver-named-service-handle-generation-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Asset Registry专项冻结47个focused文件、11,574行、419,973 bytes与74项test。当前projected meta inventory、multi-file durable journal、targeted generation和immutable catalog generation是真实底座；但duplicate GUID自动remint不迁移serialized reference closure，独立rebuild还会提前逐sidecar写入。更直接的产品边界是`RecoveryDeferred`在project/resource/watcher/generation event已经发布后才被转成错误，形成失败返回与live成功并存。registry row/persistence缺exact type/schema/artifact/source/generation/currentness，query仍全表scan/分配排序，public registry incremental helper只有tests caller。报告登记2项P0、60项P1、16项P2与40项资格门；Runtime04/24/25、Editor04、Tooling37与PERF-MVP-556继续拥有共享父问题。详见[zircon_runtime/51-runtime-asset-registry-index-persistence-rebuild-incremental-query-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/51-runtime-asset-registry-index-persistence-rebuild-incremental-query-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Dynamic Scene Session Archive专项冻结565个production文件、10,510行、360,657 bytes与7项内部test，另读25个direct test文件和19个参考文件。dense slot/index、lineage/revision-bound plan、不可变sealed artifact、512 MiB上限和bounded writer是真实底座；但547个public function没有普通产品consumer，path mutation的load-all覆盖没有跨进程CAS/lock，`flush()`不等于crash durability，backup/restore吞错且没有journal恢复。query每次完整解析archive，schema/metadata/merge/retention不能承载平台存档；restore又缺world epoch、安全点、participant、rollback与原子world/metadata publication。报告登记0项新增P0、60项P1、16项P2与40项资格门；Runtime04/05/22/24/25/40/41继续拥有共享父问题。详见[zircon_runtime/52-runtime-dynamic-scene-session-archive-slot-capture-restore-path-merge-retention-durability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/52-runtime-dynamic-scene-session-archive-slot-capture-restore-path-merge-retention-durability-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Dynamic Scene Asset Reload专项冻结14个production owner文件、2,966行、103,682 bytes与4项内部test，另读3个direct test、10个spawn/preflight支持文件、7个产品集成文件和15个参考文件。当前event budget、per-asset single-flight prepare、deferred successor、stale/cancel检查、gap reconcile、target generation CAS和compiled spawn transaction是真实底座；但SceneAsset事件与gap扫描没有project/world/Level/instance qualification，会把无关场景应用到当前Level。Modified/Renamed只追加spawn，Removed/ReloadFailed不清理既有实例，remap未进入持久instance/reference repair；revision又在locator/admission/schedule成功前前移，ready resident overflow只计drop，使同revision随后永久stale。报告登记3项P0、60项P1、16项P2与40项资格门；目标是qualified `DynamicSceneInstanceRegistry + SceneReloadCoordinator`及last-known-good replace transaction。详见[zircon_runtime/53-runtime-dynamic-scene-asset-reload-event-generation-reconciliation-stage-apply-instance-replacement-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/53-runtime-dynamic-scene-asset-reload-event-generation-reconciliation-stage-apply-instance-replacement-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Scene Event Mirror专项冻结10个Runtime core/World/Session owner文件、10个Interface/Gateway/Editor consumer文件、5个SDK/AI/Navigation产品文件、11个direct test文件和8个参考文件。fixed page/queue、bounded raw JSON、Session allocation rollback、generational reclaim、reader-count rollback与Editor单页公平pump是真实底座；但AI Runtime仅注册普通event，三个AI Editor mirror consumer在真实产品无法订阅。Runtime又在Gateway decode和typed callback前commit，overflow与callback failure没有连续sequence、ack、dropped range或resync；每subscriber重复observer、JSON encode和64 MiB queue，使publish CPU与RSS缺少全局上界。报告登记3项P0、60项P1、16项P2与40项资格门；目标是`SceneEventBroker + SharedEncodedStream + AcknowledgedConsumerCursor`。详见[zircon_runtime/54-runtime-scene-event-mirror-registration-subscription-cursor-backlog-overflow-reclaim-abi-consumer-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/54-runtime-scene-event-mirror-registration-subscription-cursor-backlog-overflow-reclaim-abi-consumer-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Foundation专项冻结31个Foundation contract/manager/Core config-event文件、14个产品链文件、15个direct test文件和14个参考文件。dirty/persisted generation、debounce worker、atomic writer、backup recovery、commit fence、有界flush及Core EventBus策略是真实底座；但旧磁盘值可覆盖dynamic session显式profile，任一Editor持久变更又会把整个Core Store中的session/window/platform值写入全局文件。第二个live Runtime注册同一路径会静默使第一个manager的后续commit stale；两个Immediate driver为空ZST，manager不依赖它们，EventManager生产resolver为0。报告登记3项P0、56项P1、14项P2与40项资格门；目标是`CompiledFoundationContract + LayeredConfigAuthority + TypedEventService + ScopedPersistenceBroker`。详见[zircon_runtime/55-runtime-foundation-module-config-event-service-driver-manager-persistence-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/55-runtime-foundation-module-config-event-service-driver-manager-persistence-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Input专项冻结46个contract/runtime文件、14个focused test文件、45个dynamic/App/script/sample产品链文件和20个参考文件。button edge、frame transition、compiled action generation/workspace、相邻motion合并、bounded recorder与gamepad poll budget是真实底座；但Input module以空driver和零Action/Replay产品consumer伪Ready，真实脚本与Vampire仍走raw snapshot。UI在物理状态提交前停止传播，可在capture/focus变化时吞掉单侧press/release并留下永久held；persisted Action Map又直接保存临时gilrs ID与无版本键盘Debug哈希/native code。报告登记3项P0、64项P1、16项P2与40项资格门；目标是`InputIngressBroker + QualifiedPhysicalState + CompiledActionProgram + InputOwnershipArbiter + StableDeviceBinding + DeterministicInputJournal`。详见[zircon_runtime/56-input-device-event-frame-state-action-map-focus-gamepad-recording-replay-host-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/56-input-device-event-frame-state-action-map-focus-gamepad-recording-replay-host-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Platform Host专项冻结34个Window/Platform合同文件、79个App host文件、26个dynamic ABI/surface/export文件、59个focused test文件和23个参考文件。typed descriptor、winit handler、Win32 raw surface与cadence是真实底座；但Platform disabled且没有window backend owner时仍可报Supported，App只有单窗口/固定viewport 1并丢弃WindowId，OS Destroyed或suspend又没有在原生对象失效前撤销unsafe surface。报告登记2项P0、64项P1、16项P2与40项资格门；目标是`PlatformHostService + WindowRegistry + DisplayTopologySnapshot + ApplicationLifecycleMachine + SurfaceLeaseRegistry + HostCommandBroker + EventLoopScheduler`。generated export callback假成功继续由Tooling03的`TOOL-EXPORT-P0-005`唯一拥有。详见[zircon_runtime/57-platform-host-window-registry-monitor-display-event-loop-application-lifecycle-surface-command-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/57-platform-host-window-registry-monitor-display-event-loop-application-lifecycle-surface-command-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Plugin Interface Bridge专项冻结14个core/lifecycle文件、11个native/VM文件、17个App/Editor/首方插件产品文件、19个focused test文件和14个参考文件。frozen table、generation/provider同快照、weak cache、native library callback lease与registration replay cache是真实底座；但bridge调用没有覆盖完整closure的lease，native registration replay没有产品caller，已replay旧World又会捕获旧scope并在hot reload后静默停跑、阻止旧DLL retirement。报告登记3项P0、64项P1、16项P2与40项资格门；目标是`InterfaceContractCatalog + BridgeTableGeneration + BridgeCallLease + BridgeLifecycleTransaction + WorldBridgeBindingRegistry + VmBridgeAdapter + BridgeObservationStream`。详见[zircon_runtime/58-runtime-plugin-interface-bridge-slot-generation-strong-weak-native-vm-lifecycle-diagnostics-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/58-runtime-plugin-interface-bridge-slot-generation-strong-weak-native-vm-lifecycle-diagnostics-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Task Execution专项冻结36个canonical core文件、82个production consumer/worker文件、27个focused test文件和29个参考文件。三类Rayon pool、continuation、worker协助等待、panic捕获以及带entry/byte budget、key coalescing、fence、deadline-before-start、cancel authority和terminal ticket的bounded keyed I/O lane是真实底座；但framework task模型不进入真实scheduler，TasksModule不拥有execution lifecycle，进程pool、Text与Graphics私有worker不能形成可信线程预算，handle缺typed result/owner/cancel，timer和产品shutdown也没有统一receipt。本篇不重复登记Runtime02与Editor09已有两项P0，新增72项P1、18项P2与40项资格门；目标是`ExecutionRuntime + WorkerDomain + TaskScope + TaskDescriptor + Task<T> + DependencyGraph + DeadlineService + BoundedOperationLane + DedicatedWorkerLease + ExecutionDiagnostics`。详见[zircon_runtime/59-runtime-task-execution-job-scheduler-handle-dependency-cancellation-thread-budget-timer-shutdown-diagnostics-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/59-runtime-task-execution-job-scheduler-handle-dependency-cancellation-thread-budget-timer-shutdown-diagnostics-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Scene ECS专项逐文件冻结`scene/ecs`143个Rust文件、World/Level产品接线93个文件、44个focused test文件和53个参考文件。columnar archetype、sparse set、compiled query plan、lazy change tick、packed command arena、bounded message、active event worklist与stable worker merge是真实底座；但system `Query::iter(&self)`可在首个iterator借用plan切片时再次可变刷新同一QueryState并潜在悬空slice，`RemovedComponentEvents`又按历史总量永久累积。真实World系统仍不能进入worker并行，显式`before/after`也未进入worker batch barrier。本篇登记2项P0、72项P1、18项P2与40项资格门，目标是`WorldIdentity + ComponentSchemaRegistry + ArchetypeStore + QueryPlan + WorldCell + ScheduleGraph + EcsExecutor + DeferredWorld + EventRegistry + ObserverGraph + EcsDiagnostics`。详见[zircon_runtime/60-runtime-scene-ecs-entity-component-storage-archetype-query-access-change-detection-command-schedule-parallel-event-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/60-runtime-scene-ecs-entity-component-storage-archetype-query-access-change-detection-command-schedule-parallel-event-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Scene World/Level与Project I/O专项冻结74个production文件、14,233行、526,609 bytes，沿Editor Save、Enter/Exit Play、runtime play snapshot load、canonical `.scene.toml` load/save和LevelManager save五条真实产品链逐调用点审查。World replacement epoch、DynamicScene staged commit、严格资源引用、bounded keyed I/O和单文件原子替换是真实底座；但Save成功清dirty与Exit Play都会经过有损`World::clone`，Play把快照追加到含默认实体的World，canonical SceneAsset又丢弃terrain/tilemap/prefab且没有Sprite2D/Mesh2D。报告登记5项P0、60项P1、14项P2与45项资格门，目标是`WorldContextRegistry + LevelInstanceRegistry + WorldLifecycleCoordinator + AuthoringSceneDocument + SceneSchemaRegistry + SnapshotCompiler + ScenePersistenceService + WorldReplacementTransaction`。详见[zircon_runtime/61-runtime-scene-world-level-registry-lifecycle-project-io-snapshot-clone-serialization-schema-transaction-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/61-runtime-scene-world-level-registry-lifecycle-project-io-snapshot-clone-serialization-schema-transaction-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Scene Hierarchy专项冻结49个Zircon文件、14,667行、540,223 bytes，并对20个参考引擎文件、40,518行做逐语义对照。当前公共泛型与reflection API可绕过受保护的Hierarchy、LocalTransform和Mobility约束，制造raw cycle、非法Static关系或未验证transform；WorldMatrix与ActiveInHierarchy派生真相也可被伪造/删除并直接污染renderer。reparent没有KeepWorld/Snap规则，派生更新仍以World级dirty bool全量重建，Scene又缺canonical bounds/spatial delta，Editor viewport还混合clone geometry与原World gizmo代际。报告登记2项P0、64项P1、16项P2与48项资格门，目标是protected component authority、topology transaction、增量derived frontier、participation policy和persistent spatial handoff。详见[zircon_runtime/62-runtime-scene-hierarchy-transform-propagation-reparent-activation-mobility-visibility-bounds-render-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/62-runtime-scene-hierarchy-transform-propagation-reparent-activation-mobility-visibility-bounds-render-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Resource Authority专项冻结205个去重Zircon文件、35,248行、1,257,681 bytes及25个参考文件、40,525行。当前record/revision/readiness、typed downcast、project dependency闭包、reload candidate和局部lease是真实底座；但公开`store_payload`允许把Shader精确payload写入Texture broad kind，形成broad Loaded、typed NotLoaded且resident路径拒绝自愈的三重事实；frame submission和ResourceStreamer又可同步执行冷盘依赖加载、decode与深clone。报告登记2项P0、66项P1、17项P2与50项资格门，目标是`ExactAssetTypeCatalog + QualifiedAssetHandle + AssetLoadCoordinator + ResourceVersionSlot + VersionLease + TypedDependencyGraph + ReloadCandidateTransaction + AssetPublicationReceipt`；通用artifact、GPU residency、identity、event cursor、task scheduler、Editor/importer父边界继续由Runtime04/09D/24/51/54/59、Editor04与Plugins07拥有。详见[zircon_runtime/64-runtime-resource-authority-asset-handle-load-request-state-machine-version-lease-cache-dependency-reload-cancellation-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/64-runtime-resource-authority-asset-handle-load-request-state-machine-version-lease-cache-dependency-reload-cancellation-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Scalability专项冻结196个去重Zircon文件、67,391行、2,743,743 bytes及30个参考文件、26,926行。当前profile短锁应用、capability summary/compile裁剪、frame/memory profile、degrade ladder和动态分辨率控制器数学是真实底座；但生产只读取camera固定scale，controller与GPU sample/decision无人接线，14 ms和内存预算固定，global ladder使一个viewport降级所有viewport，profile又无schema/device/benchmark/requested-effective receipt。Editor是唯一明确production caller且硬编码HGI/VG/budget，App/viewer没有统一quality policy。报告登记0项新增P0、64项P1、16项P2与48项资格门，目标是`QualityProfileCatalog + DeviceProfileResolver + CapabilityTierResolver + FrameBudgetController + DynamicResolutionCoordinator + FeatureScalabilityRegistry + PerViewportQualityState + QualityTransitionTransaction + EffectiveQualityReceipt`。详见[zircon_runtime/65-runtime-scalability-quality-profile-device-profile-capability-tier-dynamic-resolution-frame-budget-lod-feature-fallback-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/65-runtime-scalability-quality-profile-device-profile-capability-tier-dynamic-resolution-frame-budget-lod-feature-fallback-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime XR/OpenXR专项冻结719个去重Zircon文件、133,681行、4,812,247 bytes及五参考71个文件、32,858行。当前camera/ViewFamily命名底座、RHI capability、WGPU backend、window surface、input action与gamepad可作为接入点；但没有loader/instance/system/session、graphics binding、XR swapchain lease、runtime-driven wait/begin/locate/end时序、per-view pose/FOV/history、reference space、OpenXR action、late update、composition layer、foveation或产品/Editor/cook闭环，119处`multiview_mask`赋值全部为`None`。报告登记0项新增P0、72项P1、16项P2与48项资格门，目标是`XrRuntimeProvider + XrInstanceAuthority + XrSystemProfile + XrSessionSupervisor + XrFramePacer + XrSpaceGraph + XrActionRuntime + XrGraphicsBindingBridge + XrSwapchainLease + XrViewFamily + XrLateUpdateCoordinator + XrCompositionLayerGraph + XrExtensionRegistry + XrDiagnosticsReceipt`。详见[zircon_runtime/66-runtime-xr-openxr-device-session-stereo-view-tracking-input-late-update-foveation-compositor-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/66-runtime-xr-openxr-device-session-stereo-view-tracking-input-late-update-foveation-compositor-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Console/CVar专项冻结523个去重Zircon文件、45,106行、1,581,380 bytes及五参考32个文件、26,526行。当前raw JSON ConfigStore/Foundation persistence、bounded RuntimeOperationService、diagnostic log与Editor Console输出面板、Editor command registry和catalog/profile是可复用底座，但第一方production精确搜索只有一条Editor测试文本命中，没有runtime command/CVar registry、typed layer/effective receipt、principal/permission、cheat/shipping gate、replication/remote gateway或App/Editor/headless交互接线。报告登记0项新增P0、72项P1、16项P2与48项资格门，目标是`RuntimeControlRegistry + ConsoleCommandCatalog + ConsoleVariableCatalog + ConsoleVariableLayerStack + ConsoleMutationTransaction + ConsoleApplyScheduler + ConsolePrincipalPolicy + ConsoleSession + ConsoleOutputStream + ConsoleHistoryStore + RemoteControlGateway + ConsoleDiagnosticsReceipt`。详见[zircon_runtime/67-runtime-console-command-cvar-registry-cheat-exec-config-layer-replication-remote-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/67-runtime-console-command-cvar-registry-cheat-exec-config-layer-replication-remote-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Sprite2D/Canvas2D专项冻结132个去重Zircon文件、28,208行、1,047,645 bytes及五参考69个文件、42,763行。当前Sprite2D能实际提交像素，TileSet/TileMap已有typed asset链，Tilemap插件也诚实报告Partial；但Sprite bounds/sort/material/phase语义没有闭合，renderer逐batch每帧建buffer/开pass，SpriteAtlas不是runtime asset/artifact，Mesh2D无render consumer，TileMap没有component、chunk compiler、renderer、projection、lighting、physics/navigation、streaming或mutation receipt。报告登记0项新增P0、72项P1、16项P2与48项资格门；Runtime61与Editor34继续唯一拥有持久化丢失和2D authoring/schema硬阻断。详见[zircon_runtime/68-runtime-sprite2d-canvas2d-sprite-atlas-tileset-tilemap-batching-sorting-lighting-physics-streaming-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/68-runtime-sprite2d-canvas2d-sprite-atlas-tileset-tilemap-batching-sorting-lighting-physics-streaming-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Mesh3D专项冻结278个去重Zircon文件、59,135行、2,166,523 bytes及五参考37个文件、50,833行。typed Mesh attributes/index/morph、Scene primitive/material/LOD、真实GPU Scene history、cached command/indirect replay及skin/morph PNG是可保留底座；但Mesh没有stable section/material slot/LOD artifact，Scene同时暴露四类geometry authority，LOD只按translation距离，产品每draw固定一个instance，GPU skin/morph预付CPU变形，delta/palette无共享arena，render prepare同步load且collision无产品cook。报告新增1项P0，确认material overrides、tint与alpha mode可渲染却不进入Scene save/reopen；另登记48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门，共享bounds/instancing、同步冷加载、physics cook与inverse-bind/skeleton阻断继续由既有唯一owner持有。详见[zircon_runtime/69-runtime-mesh-static-mesh-skeletal-mesh-submesh-lod-instancing-skinning-morph-collision-streaming-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/69-runtime-mesh-static-mesh-skeletal-mesh-submesh-lod-instancing-skinning-morph-collision-streaming-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Scene Text专项冻结470个去重Zircon文件、107,076行、3,757,609 bytes及五参考19个文件、9,405行。Font asset/import/load、shared shaping/layout/glyph artifact与UI atlas/SDF/GPU framebuffer是可保留底座；但NodeKind、Scene asset/record/snapshot/project I/O/reflection/property/render extract和Editor create/inspect均无SceneText，现有UI batch绑定tree/node/UiFrame/screen rect且全部text pipeline无depth，不能加transform冒充Text3D。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门；Runtime11B/11C、09B/09C/09D、43、61/62/63/64/65、Editor03/05/33、App06与Runtime09 UI bridge failure继续唯一拥有父问题。详见[zircon_runtime/70-runtime-scene-text-text2d-text3d-billboard-font-layout-localization-extract-render-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/70-runtime-scene-text-text2d-text3d-billboard-font-layout-localization-extract-render-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Scene Light专项冻结53个Zircon文件、17,796行、659,163 bytes及五参考31个文件、14,862行。五类light component/asset、typed ECS、project I/O、reflection、snapshot与Editor create/inspect是可保留底座；但Spot部分资产会静默采用+Y、百万强度和零锥角，单一NodeKind又可隐藏并同时渲染五种light component；photometry、schema migration、transform authority、shadow/cookie/IES dependency、delta extract与完整Editor handles均未闭合。报告登记2项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与48项资格门；Runtime09E继续唯一拥有cluster/shadow/area-light renderer父P0，Runtime61/62/63/64/65与Editor03/05/22保留共享父边界。详见[zircon_runtime/71-runtime-scene-light-directional-point-spot-rect-photometry-layer-shadow-cookie-ies-extract-authoring-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/71-runtime-scene-light-directional-point-spot-rect-photometry-layer-shadow-cookie-ies-extract-authoring-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Network专项冻结Net全包186个文件、14,377行、495,870 bytes与120项测试。base socket、HTTP/WS和局部RUDP/RPC/Replication/Download算法真实存在，但普通runtime catalog只装配root manager，六个feature provider均不进入产品；HTTP/WS创建私有manager，Content Download生产路径反而解析不到其HTTP backend。Net Editor未进catalog、5个资源缺失、6个operation无factory，NativeDynamic dist也只投影metadata。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立per-world/session唯一owner、feature activation、真实transport/RPC/Replication/Installer、Editor topology与包装形态parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime08E、Editor26、Plugins01/06等canonical owner负责。详见[zircon_plugins/10-first-party-network-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-transport-rpc-replication-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/10-first-party-network-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-transport-rpc-replication-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Particle/VFX专项冻结Particles全包57个文件、8,733行、306,329 bytes与41项测试。CPU/GPU底座真实存在，但runtime catalog已链接而editor catalog未链接；12个领域operation无event/factory且菜单全disabled，三个ZUI只有占位控件，NativeDynamic dist也只能投影metadata。manager CPU fallback、renderer GPU owner和Scene dynamic JSON仍是三条事实源，Render Graph compute executor不记录真实dispatch。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与32项资格门，要求建立单一source artifact、per-world runtime、真实graph dispatch、Editor preview和source/native parity；硬阻塞继续由Runtime26、Editor15/50、Plugins01/06等canonical owner负责。详见[zircon_plugins/09-first-party-particle-vfx-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-simulation-render-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/09-first-party-particle-vfx-source-runtime-editor-dist-catalog-simulation-render-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Editor Authoring Extension专项冻结Material、Animation Graph、Timeline、UI Asset、Prefab、Tilemap、Terrain、Texture与Runtime Diagnostics九包112个文件，并追踪共享catalog/host/caller 30个文件。九包77项测试没有覆盖真实default-host挂载：42个命令中30个领域操作没有event/factory，3个UI创建命令只OpenView，20条plugin文档URI全部缺文件；editor catalog对九包0链接，runtime catalog对四个runtime-backed包只链接Texture，九个native dist也无法重建source行为。报告登记0项新增P0、72项P1、18项P2与32项资格门，要求统一package/resource/operation/document/mount合同及source/native parity，并把领域实现收敛到既有Editor/Runtime canonical owner。详见[zircon_plugins/08-first-party-editor-authoring-extension-document-operation-toolkit-runtime-contract-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/08-first-party-editor-authoring-extension-document-operation-toolkit-runtime-contract-product-integration-review.md)。

First-Party Asset Importer专项冻结七个插件根115个文件，并向下追踪Runtime内建importer、registry、ProjectAssetManager、first-party catalog与App caller共138个文件。Texture已有DDS/KTX/ASTC结构校验与BC5/mip底座，Runtime glTF也已有真实AnimationClip/Skeleton与labeled subasset；但tracked Texture mip kernel存在编译阻断，catalog stable glTF会以placeholder遮蔽更完整的内建实现，glTF/OBJ index、external URI snapshot/containment和KTX2解压预算均未fail-close。报告登记5项P0、72项P1、16项P2与20项资格门，要求唯一canonical importer、SourceBroker、stable SubassetKey、完整platform cook和真实产品闭环。详见[zircon_plugins/07-first-party-asset-importer-source-dependency-subasset-artifact-determinism-sandbox-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/07-first-party-asset-importer-source-dependency-subasset-artifact-determinism-sandbox-product-integration-review.md)。

Runtime Interface契约测试专项确认crate内已有401项测试和大量本机layout/serde/DTO行为基础，但required验证没有正向加载构建出的真实Runtime DLL；常规路径主要调用同进程链接符号、fake table或系统库missing-symbol fixture，也没有生成式C header/consumer、BuildSet skew、历史golden corpus、property/fuzz、unload/reload和故障隔离资格。唯一package-root integration文件只验证UI binding parser，不能替代二进制或跨语言认证。发布前必须建立InterfaceSpec、ABI/symbol/layout manifest及五层C1-C5认证，而不能用same-version round-trip或源码`.contains()`守卫声称稳定。详见[zircon_runtime_interface/07-contract-certification-abi-layout-version-skew-cross-language-fuzz-test-architecture-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/07-contract-certification-abi-layout-version-skew-cross-language-fuzz-test-architecture-review.md)。

[zircon_runtime/46-engine-module-service-contract-context-factory-descriptor-snapshot-composition-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/46-engine-module-service-contract-context-factory-descriptor-snapshot-composition-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md)拥有Engine Module/Service Contract专项证据：保留Core依赖图冻结、module lifecycle、service声明校验与运行态快照，重构module/descriptor双身份、Runtime→App→Core多代descriptor生成、report与注册binding分叉、无生产consumer且可矛盾的`EngineService`合同、Core/VM PluginContext身份权限混用，以及lazy factory panic跳过slot reset/notify的P0；开放的descriptor regeneration failure继续保持open。

[zircon_runtime/45-preference-settings-scope-storage-overlay-bounded-io-generation-fence-durability-migration-multi-process-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/45-preference-settings-scope-storage-overlay-bounded-io-generation-fence-durability-migration-multi-process-product-integration-review.md)拥有Preference/Settings专项证据：保留中立storage、read-your-write overlay、双容量admission、generation coalescing、fence accounting和shared atomic writer，重构未flush write误报Durable、WOC无生产refresh/harvest owner、hung backend冻结全lane且drop无界等待、固定root无product/principal隔离、跨进程无revision/CAS/watch以及Editor第二durable owner；Framework05两项与Runtime11 failure继续保持open。

[zircon_runtime/44-process-diagnostic-log-router-filter-record-queue-sink-durability-rotation-crash-multi-session-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/44-process-diagnostic-log-router-filter-record-queue-sink-durability-rotation-crash-multi-session-product-integration-review.md)拥有Process Diagnostic Log专项证据：保留compiled filter、lazy callsite、有界count queue、batch worker、metrics、flush/shutdown和dynamic session lease，重构App/DLL双router、无schema/sequence/context record、无限阻塞critical admission、console/file耦合、非耐久flush、无rotation/retention/manifest、panic payload缺失及Editor/tracing/plugin多事实源；Runtime07 synchronous-sink failure继续保持open。

[zircon_runtime/43-dynamic-runtime-session-registry-ffi-frame-event-extract-host-request-world-sync-ui-shader-prewarm-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/43-dynamic-runtime-session-registry-ffi-frame-event-extract-host-request-world-sync-ui-shader-prewarm-product-integration-review.md)拥有Dynamic Runtime Session Control Plane专项证据：保留V7 panic边界、quiescence、foreign owner、bounded JSON、分页与extract诊断，重构全局裸handle、单session锁、无限destroy、false-success frame/a11y、项目启动旁路、输入/wake/page identity、产品HUD/menu污染和shader prewarm身份/调度。

LiveOps控制面的首轮E3审查确认八张Workbench只存在于DesignSpec、PNG与说明文档，生产Editor、Runtime、Plugin和Hub没有对应capability、document、operation、provider或policy consumer；页面却固定宣称`Live v42`、12个live flags、42K users、3个live experiments、184 crashes与92% resolved，renderer没有事件、网络、存储、计时器或签名路径。必须先将这些页面降级为Prototype/Unavailable，再建立signed immutable policy snapshot、deterministic runtime evaluation、review/publish/rollout、consent-qualified segment/experiment，并只编排BuildSet、entitlement、install与crash的canonical owner。详见[zircon_editor/46-liveops-feature-flag-remote-config-segmentation-experiment-patch-dlc-crash-control-plane-authoring-review.md](zircon_editor/46-liveops-feature-flag-remote-config-segmentation-experiment-patch-dlc-crash-control-plane-authoring-review.md)。

Editor Runtime gateway/session消费链的首轮E3审查确认ArcSwap immutable snapshot、RuntimeSession强owner、有界plugin page、round-robin与indexed WorldWatchMap真实存在；但World Sync先读generation再由另一个snapshot drain，event consumer也不保存subscription的origin gateway/session identity。Runtime replacement时，复用的opaque token可跨项目或PIE session投影/取消；world page又没有cursor/final/gap合同，bus拒绝仍会推进watermark，插件callback panic可泄漏subscription或丢当前delivery，Host也没有统一terminal shutdown。必须先建立session-qualified lease与replacement barrier，再完成page commit/resync、consumer fault domain、断线本地退休和显式shutdown。详见[zircon_editor/47-runtime-gateway-session-event-consumer-world-sync-generation-backpressure-reconnect-shutdown-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/47-runtime-gateway-session-event-consumer-world-sync-generation-backpressure-reconnect-shutdown-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Message Bus的首轮E3审查确认共享`Arc` delivery、per-subscriber inbox、三类retention、双重bytes预算、checked identity/sequence、lossless预检和锁外request handler已经存在；但`view.invalidated`只有在至少一个subscriber实际收到消息时才标记dirty，而生产没有该topic consumer，合法发布会静默退化为“成功但不刷新”。同时，publish在backpressure后已消费消息、unregister可静默销毁lossless inbox、dispatch计划没有subscription generation、drain没有ack/resync，plugin bridge又把有界inbox转存到无界pending队列。必须先拆分view dirty authority与message delivery，再建立owner-qualified lease、non-consuming admission、typed disposition、page/ack/resync及terminal shutdown。详见[zircon_editor/48-editor-message-bus-topic-subscription-inbox-retention-admission-dispatch-request-dirty-projection-shutdown-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/48-editor-message-bus-topic-subscription-inbox-retention-admission-dispatch-request-dirty-projection-shutdown-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Event Runtime的首轮E3审查确认sequence/revision分离、共享record owner、三类有界retention、latest index、listener cursor和锁外route基础已经存在；但F5 retained automation把global journal长度当作单binding因果receipt，retention/coalesce后可越界或误归因，随后又把RetainedHost记录改写成CLI source与硬编码binding。raw replay会重新执行输入、失败和外部副作用，pointer move仍支付完整审计链，revision又在执行前推进。必须先用typed Action/Execution/Publication Receipt替代snapshot长度差，再拆分realtime input、audit observation与committed replay，建立qualified revision、listener generation/page/ack/resync及终态生命周期。详见[zircon_editor/49-editor-event-runtime-envelope-listener-registry-journal-replay-snapshot-dirty-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/49-editor-event-runtime-envelope-listener-registry-journal-replay-snapshot-dirty-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Extension Runtime的首轮E3审查确认immutable contribution snapshot、ticket revoke、有界change journal、scene/overlay panic boundary和toolkit save/close lease已经存在；但Plugin Manager的active generation没有Workbench consumer，RetainedHost startup又直接安装另一套永久状态。产品无统一revoke/quiesce，scene/overlay/importer capability规则可旁路，Inspector/pane callback缺隔离且可在shell锁内执行，DocumentToolkitRegistry还在mutex内调用trait并拆裂dirty authority。必须建立唯一ExtensionReconciler、owner-generation mount lease、跨registry prepare/commit/revoke、effective capability和callback supervisor。详见[zircon_editor/50-editor-extension-contribution-store-registry-toolkit-provider-snapshot-reload-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/50-editor-extension-contribution-store-registry-toolkit-provider-snapshot-reload-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Project Lifecycle的首轮E3审查确认staged create、canonical open、SessionGuard、Hub launch/focus mailbox与activation rollback入口已经存在；但项目派生plugin可在exclusive admission和Engine/BuildSet兼容批准前加载，rollback关闭runtime project失败后仍释放guard，Claimed/Activating lease被当成Ready，focus watcher不会随Welcome后Open/项目切换重绑，Ready又早于native window与first present。必须建立data-only preflight、admission lifecycle、可恢复activation/close transaction、generation-qualified focus ack、first-present receipt与Safe/Recovery Mode。详见[zircon_editor/51-editor-project-startup-open-create-authority-hub-handshake-session-guard-focus-recent-recovery-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/51-editor-project-startup-open-create-authority-hub-handshake-session-guard-focus-recent-recovery-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Builtin View/Window Catalog的首轮E3审查确认40项集中目录、View/Window placement、14个pane template、8个activity-window template和UI Asset/Animation真实session样板已经存在；但19项仍落入Placeholder，17个所谓functional panel/window没有content provider，10个panel只有默认布局caller，Scene/Game Window没有目录外caller，四个feature window只有command/menu入口，UI Asset与Asset Browser window也只多一个Welcome入口，Prefab正文又明确仍是placeholder。必须先把假Available入口降级，再建立kind-specific typed definition、compiled immutable catalog、provider-bound open transaction、复合capability、template/localization/icon link与40项产品资格矩阵。详见[zircon_editor/52-editor-builtin-view-window-descriptor-catalog-content-provider-capability-template-localization-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/52-editor-builtin-view-window-descriptor-catalog-content-provider-capability-template-localization-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Interactive Tool Scheduler的首轮E3审查确认canonical resource set、single FIFO、atomic set request、typed outcome/event及真实SceneModeStack生命周期已经存在；但service只有构造caller，SceneMode、Viewport、Gizmo、Modal、Export和extension unload均绕过租约，`editor.tool`没有subscriber。同一ToolId还可用新集合覆盖active set并遗留holder，state解锁后逐条publish会在并发下形成与最终authority相反的event顺序。必须建立Definition/Instance/Request/Lease identity、scoped ResourceKey、revisioned transition/outbox、input capture、terminal disposition及真实产品adapter。详见[zircon_editor/53-editor-interactive-tool-scheduler-resource-lease-input-capture-mode-modal-extension-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/53-editor-interactive-tool-scheduler-resource-lease-input-capture-mode-modal-extension-lifecycle-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Workbench Shell AutoLayout的首轮E3审查确认typed skeleton/region/tier/constraint、componentized Taffy tree、drawer resize和89项聚焦测试已经存在；但tracked `shell_regions.toml`、`WorkbenchShellRegionsAsset`和CSS-like parser没有production consumer，产品仍从hardcoded skeleton与ZUI template构造。一次recompute还同时维护custom legacy geometry与真实template frames，两者responsive/compact规则不同，且template失败后只记日志并把旧frames与新model/legacy geometry混合发布。必须建立versioned source compiler、recursive RegionGraph、provider/token link、单一Taffy geometry authority和同代原子publication。详见[zircon_editor/54-editor-workbench-shell-autolayout-constraint-language-responsive-region-binding-geometry-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/54-editor-workbench-shell-autolayout-constraint-language-responsive-region-binding-geometry-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Structured Clipboard的首轮E3审查确认Delete已有根选择归一化、camera预检、单transaction与selection恢复，Runtime也提供exact `DetachedEntityBatch`和带`EntityRemap`的DynamicScene底座；但Editor没有Copy/Cut/Paste/Duplicate command、keymap、menu、intent或service，hierarchy drag仍以字符串reference和旁路NodeId数组为双权威。现存Delete command更会丢弃exact detached batch并只按固定`NodeRecord`撤销，静默丢失任意typed/dynamic/plugin component、observer与tick/storage状态。必须先关闭该数据完整性P0，再建立domain provider、portable payload、subset capture、clone/remap policy、destination plan、OS clipboard和drag convergence。详见[zircon_editor/55-editor-structured-clipboard-cut-copy-paste-duplicate-delete-cross-document-remap-drag-payload-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/55-editor-structured-clipboard-cut-copy-paste-duplicate-delete-cross-document-remap-drag-payload-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Search/Filter/Find Usage的首轮E3审查确认Asset Browser和Hierarchy存在真实可用的局部过滤基础，Hierarchy还有祖先保留、Unicode fallback与5k规模测试；但Asset只对当前目录做ASCII子串+单kind线性扫描，Hierarchy active filter会禁用稀疏fragment并全量reflow，Blend Space只筛三个模板常量。更严重的是Workbench Scene搜索无events、Effect/Tags route被吞成no-op，Icon Find Usage固定显示14 references，Gameplay Tags Reference Scan只选择静态route。必须先关闭两项能力真实性P0，再建立typed query、provider/index generation、cancelable paged result、qualified target、stale校验与navigation receipt。详见[zircon_editor/56-editor-search-filter-query-index-result-find-usage-reference-navigation-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/56-editor-search-filter-query-index-result-find-usage-reference-navigation-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Asset Workspace/Content Browser的首轮E3审查冻结209份Zircon文件、33,959行与250项test attribute。catalog/details/preview generation、稳定folder排序、enabled Asset Type Registry、operation write-target准入和typed asset drag payload是真实可保留底座；但`Locate selected asset`不携带资产且只打开Assets pane，item单击只有selection而无双击/Enter/OpenAsset生产路由，catalog又把插件exact `AssetTypeId`压成`ResourceKind`，使自定义toolkit/context/presentation不可恢复。更严重的是default scene watcher会重新scan/load并直接replace dirty authoring world。必须先关闭四项P0，再建立per-instance Browser state、content-source provider、activation/action provider、qualified mutation/change receipt、history/favorites/collections和大规模paged product面。详见[zircon_editor/57-editor-asset-workspace-content-browser-folder-source-tree-selection-open-create-import-rename-move-delete-history-collection-product-integration-review.md](zircon_editor/57-editor-asset-workspace-content-browser-folder-source-tree-selection-open-create-import-rename-move-delete-history-collection-product-integration-review.md)。

Editor Scene Viewport Host/Render Product的首轮E3审查冻结86份Zircon文件、11,974行与109项test attribute。真实viewport lifecycle、scene extract、direct GPU external image、CPU capture、generation-keyed cache和retryable native surface present是可保留底座；但所有Scene/Game/duplicate/floating pane共享一个controller、size和global image，失败会消费dirty并让旧图无stale标识地冻结，world-space UI只实现screen rectangle，runtime直出还在generation复核前发布产品。报告要求先关闭四项P0，再建立per-view session、source-qualified request/receipt、atomic target recovery、presentation lease、多Scene/Game/window隔离和真实world UI。详见[zircon_editor/58-editor-scene-viewport-host-render-product-surface-lifecycle-frame-currentness-multi-viewport-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_editor/58-editor-scene-viewport-host-render-product-surface-lifecycle-frame-currentness-multi-viewport-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Editor Scene Viewport Interaction Controller的当前源码增量深审冻结170份Zircon文件、14,877行与129项test attribute。Scene Mode先行路由、双域多选、preview到单command事务、显式Cancel命令和renderer-visible broad phase是真实底座；当前highlight DTO与Frame Selection也已分别覆盖多选集合和多选位置。但host把Pointer Cancel明确映射成无viewport command，单一drag slot会让右/中键覆盖Handle并被workbench误判为正常Commit，可选Highlight提交失败又会返回无base submission并消费render dirty。报告新增3项P0、8项P1、6项P2与36项资格门，要求收敛qualified input/capture、terminal receipt、Selectable Spatial Product、runtime Picking authority和revisioned Highlight消费，同时不重复登记Editor03/53/58、Runtime47与Runtime10开放handoff。详见[zircon_editor/59-editor-scene-viewport-interaction-controller-input-picking-selection-highlight-gizmo-transaction-cancel-generation-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_editor/59-editor-scene-viewport-interaction-controller-input-picking-selection-highlight-gizmo-transaction-cancel-generation-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Editor Scene Hierarchy / World Outliner的当前源码增量深审冻结77份Zircon文件、17,566行与157项test attribute。generation-scoped inspection artifact、稀疏name patch、fragment gap后的权威reflow、filter外selection、top-level selection归一化、transaction rollback和visible-row paint bound是真实底座；但primary press无需threshold/capture就武装reparent，旧World的drag/rename状态又会在replacement后作用于新World复用的同值NodeId。最终pane仍把row压成`id/name/depth/selected`，expanded实际等于has-children，所谓virtual rows仍按总量clone control，drop也没有typed preflight、before/after顺序、transform/owner/reference政策。报告登记2项P0、24项P1、8项P2与40项资格门，要求在Editor41 canonical owner下建立qualified item/provider/mode/column/filter、per-document view state、interaction lifecycle、immutable reparent plan和bounded row pool。详见[zircon_editor/60-editor-scene-hierarchy-outliner-tree-projection-expansion-selection-rename-reparent-drag-drop-visibility-lock-multi-world-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_editor/60-editor-scene-hierarchy-outliner-tree-projection-expansion-selection-rename-reparent-drag-drop-visibility-lock-multi-world-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Editor Scene Document / Authoring World lifecycle的当前源码增量深审冻结50份Zircon文件、14,781行与134项test attribute。Runtime显式target atomic writer、create staging/catalog rollback、document lifecycle和recovery DTO是真实底座；但startup World没有scene identity，Save Project固定写default target，Open/New/Close/Exit/watcher/autosave各自绕过统一dirty/transition authority。Editor02/03/57拥有的5项P0保持Open且不重复计数；报告新增28项P1、8项P2与40个资格门，要求建立SceneDocumentSessionRegistry、可回滚transition、expected-revision save receipt、external conflict reconciler和per-scene history/recovery。详见[zircon_editor/61-editor-scene-document-authoring-world-open-new-reload-save-close-dirty-transition-autosave-recovery-multi-document-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_editor/61-editor-scene-document-authoring-world-open-new-reload-save-close-dirty-transition-autosave-recovery-multi-document-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。
