Transaction Atomicity控制面的首轮E3审查以13,438个production-like候选文件为发现域，深入核对durable resource/asset迁移、scene/ECS、Editor transaction与scene route、Session archive、Hub目录创建、plugin/module/UI reload、RPC/HTTP retry、Coordinator数据库外effect和render submit。Resource intent journal、scene/bundle preflight、Editor apply/revert恢复及Coordinator request journal是正向基础；但多处仍会在commit前后留下partial或unknown outcome。报告建立OperationId/Intent、PreparedOperation、CommitPoint、Disposition、Compensation/Recovery、DedupStore、OperationReceipt与TransactionQualification，并明确best-effort queue与commit-only GPU submit不得伪装成rollbackable transaction。详见[zircon_tooling/37-transaction-atomicity-prepare-commit-publish-rollback-compensation-idempotency-crash-recovery-review.md](zircon_tooling/37-transaction-atomicity-prepare-commit-publish-rollback-compensation-idempotency-crash-recovery-review.md)。

Zr语言核心首轮E3审查确认外部实现已有完整parser、SemIR/AOT/LSP和128个CTest基础，但Zircon real backend依赖未固定兄弟仓库与本机DLL，required CI没有构建该后端；`.zro` reader读取却不验证signature、major/minor、native width、instruction width和endian，文件长度又能直接驱动递归分配。incremental key遗漏compiler/options/target/ABI/dependency inputs并逐文件覆盖最终artifact；类型系统的任意整数转换、复杂cast `TO_STRING`、prototype-less object copy与PIC corruption自愈还跨越checker、codegen、object和GC。详见[zircon_runtime/21-zr-language-parser-type-system-semir-bytecode-package-loader-vm-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/21-zr-language-parser-type-system-semir-bytecode-package-loader-vm-runtime-review.md)。

Time/Determinism横向审查确认Real/Virtual/Fixed三时钟、稳定schedule和deterministic worker merge可保留，但`WorldDriver`把raw real delta传给普通Update/PostUpdate，使pause、time scale和hitch clamp只影响Fixed；Fixed clock又在任何子步执行前预推进整批steps，导致每个子步读取相同最终tick/elapsed，失败时仍虚报全部时间已提交。AI、Particles与WOC的局部RNG没有共享algorithm/stream/state/replay合同。报告建立ClockDomain Registry、FrameTimeSnapshot、逐step SimulationTick transaction、RandomStream schema、checkpoint/replay/divergence和跨平台资格链。详见[zircon_runtime/22-time-clock-domain-fixed-step-determinism-rng-replay-scheduling-review.md](zircon_runtime/22-time-clock-domain-fixed-step-determinism-rng-replay-scheduling-review.md)。

Coordinate/Precision横向审查确认共享math alias、render precision seam和scene finite校验是真实基础，但`Real`仍只有f32 profile，reflection/animation/sound/render DTO让f64切换跨越schema与ABI；仓内没有World Unit/typed Space、large-world origin/rebase或per-view render-relative current/previous pipeline。Scene还接受非单位quaternion与任意近零scale，通用inverse/look-at/projection也没有fallible numeric contract。报告建立O17 Coordinate/Unit/Precision owner、canonical WorldPosition、origin generation/rebase transaction、checked narrowing与资格矩阵。详见[zircon_runtime/23-coordinate-space-unit-precision-transform-numeric-robustness-large-world-review.md](zircon_runtime/23-coordinate-space-unit-precision-transform-numeric-robustness-large-world-review.md)。

Stable Identity横向审查确认Resource UUID、ECS内部generation、脚本HostRegistry、Render Graph builder generation、字体snapshot和root lease等局部基础真实存在，但持久ID、live handle、owner-scoped handle、sequence与revision没有统一分类或codec/耗尽合同。`stable_uuid_from_components`依赖未承诺持久稳定的`DefaultHasher`，Scene load又以`max(EntityId)+1`在MAX处panic或回绕；particles等owner还存在饱和后覆盖live entry，message则在ID耗尽时panic。报告要求以O03为owner建立Identity Schema Registry、OwnerEpoch、checked allocator/slot retirement、ephemeral serialization deny、stable-to-live remap与cross-owner资格矩阵。详见[zircon_runtime/24-stable-identity-handle-generation-owner-epoch-stale-reference-exhaustion-review.md](zircon_runtime/24-stable-identity-handle-generation-owner-epoch-stale-reference-exhaustion-review.md)。

Filesystem横向审查确认`ProjectPaths`的operation/display identity、project/package root admission、durable transaction、bounded watcher和generation publication均可保留，但`ResourceIo`没有实现或consumer，immediate `AssetIoDriver`为空，固定`ResourceScheme`也没有source/mount registry、capability、generation或teardown。Runtime asset path会静默丢弃root/parent component，physical path到URI与`AssetManager::open_project(&str)`存在lossy边界，safe-file check与实际open之间又缺handle-relative no-follow能力。报告建立Path Schema、Physical Identity、Mount Registry、FileSystem Provider、secure root、Watch outcome与I/O qualification链。详见[zircon_runtime/25-filesystem-path-uri-vfs-mount-watch-sandbox-atomic-io-review.md](zircon_runtime/25-filesystem-path-uri-vfs-mount-watch-sandbox-atomic-io-review.md)。

Particle/VFX runtime首轮E3审查确认SoA/free-list、局部确定seed、GPU ping-pong/indirect draw和CPU previous-state/velocity是可保留基础，同时揭示无产品tick、JSON sprite旁路、CPU fallback/GPU owner双权威、VFX Graph no-op、aggregate重建与renderer/scalability差距。该报告冻结于旧源码；其中“diagnostic无界/每次snapshot深拷贝”已被当前工作树修复，“多实例dt被全局max污染”已被99d撤回，其余条目由99d重新归并。历史报告见[zircon_runtime/26-particle-vfx-system-emitter-cpu-gpu-simulation-rendering-scalability-determinism-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/26-particle-vfx-system-emitter-cpu-gpu-simulation-rendering-scalability-determinism-product-integration-review.md)。

Particle/VFX当前源码专项确认真实GPU compute、alive/indirect透明绘制、CPU history/velocity、共享snapshot和有界分页diagnostic可保留；但`ParticlesManager::tick`仍无产品scheduler，typed component仍不进入Scene持久化，Vampire仍写JSON最终sprite，CPU fallback和renderer GPU owner仍为双simulation authority。真实GPU compute在runtime prepare中先于Render Graph录制，三个graph compute executor只校验metadata；VFX Graph另有固定`[1,1,1]`与双no-op executor。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与44项资格门，目标是唯一`CompiledParticleProgram -> ParticleWorldRuntime -> CPU/GPU authority -> ParticleRenderPacket -> Render Graph`链。详见[zircon_runtime/99d-runtime-particle-vfx-system-emitter-cpu-gpu-simulation-rendering-scalability-determinism-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/99d-runtime-particle-vfx-system-emitter-cpu-gpu-simulation-rendering-scalability-determinism-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

Sprite2D/Canvas2D当前源码专项确认Sprite component/extract/phase、CPU tiled/sliced geometry、真实WGPU texture submit、Atlas validator以及TileSet/TileMap typed import/cache/load是真实基础，并纠正旧“Sprite完全未进入visibility”：当前已进入membership/upload planning，但输入没有2D bounds、camera rect或spatial index。产品链仍无Canvas2d/Camera2d/Light2d/AnimatedSprite/TileMapComponent，Scene project I/O丢Sprite2D/Mesh2D/TileMap，Sprite material未被streamer/batch/pipeline/shader消费，Opaque/Mask/Blend共用固定blend管线，Core2d每batch创建buffer和render pass，Atlas不是runtime resource，TileMap只到DTO/descriptor且Editor命令无handler、两个ZUI文档不存在。报告登记0项新增P0、48项P1、12项P2与44项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/99e-runtime-sprite2d-canvas2d-sprite-atlas-tileset-tilemap-batching-sorting-lighting-physics-streaming-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/99e-runtime-sprite2d-canvas2d-sprite-atlas-tileset-tilemap-batching-sorting-lighting-physics-streaming-product-integration-current-source-review.md)。

SSAO/GTAO首轮E3审查确认真实generic compute、Render Graph资源声明、shared HZB和AO history纹理可以保留，但默认shader只是8邻域raw depth差与world-space `r`normal.z`压暗；默认Forward+没有normal writer却仍绑定retained normal，terminal post process又把`AO^2`乘到完整lit scene color。现有产品测试仅要求平均亮度下降，因而会奖励错误输入与错误合成。报告登记3项P0，并要求硬切为projection-aware GTAO/VBAO、edge-aware spatial、motion/depth/normal-qualified temporal、bilateral upscale、indirect-light/specular-occlusion分离和质量/产品资格链。详见[zircon_runtime/27-screen-space-ambient-occlusion-gtao-denoise-temporal-depth-normal-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/27-screen-space-ambient-occlusion-gtao-denoise-temporal-depth-normal-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Hardware Ray Tracing首轮E3审查确认当前只有四字段`AccelerationStructureCaps`、两套零pass feature和诚实返回Unavailable的Solari provider；`RenderDevice`/CommandList/Pipeline/ShaderStage没有AS resource、build/update/compact、ray dispatch、ray stages或SBT，WGPU又无条件关闭能力且没有第二个backend。Hybrid GI硬件route只是没有AS binding的bitmask，Sound的RayTraced状态只接收外部IR samples。报告登记0项P0、56项P1、14项P2，并要求先建立capability conformance、BLAS/TLAS generation/lifetime、Ray Query/Pipeline/SBT，再接effect、denoiser、fallback和产品证据。详见[zircon_runtime/28-hardware-ray-tracing-blas-tlas-ray-query-pipeline-sbt-denoising-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/28-hardware-ray-tracing-blas-tlas-ray-query-pipeline-sbt-denoising-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Terrain/Landscape运行时首轮E3审查确认当前只有内联高度数组、单一scene引用、diagnostic importer与零phase/零pass Terrain descriptor；scene load不消费terrain，save固定写回`None`，render extract没有terrain payload。Jolt把height field展开成三角形集合，builtin physics对其query静默失败，navigation提交固定quad或跳过asset-backed geometry；foliage、partition/HLOD、联合scalability与真实产品输出也不存在。报告登记0项P0、62项P1、14项P2；既有产品阻断继续由Editor16拥有，本篇要求建立artifact/runtime instance、patch LOD/material、physics/nav、foliage/partition和44项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/29-terrain-landscape-heightfield-quadtree-lod-material-layer-foliage-world-partition-physics-navigation-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/29-terrain-landscape-heightfield-quadtree-lod-material-layer-foliage-world-partition-physics-navigation-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Water运行时首轮E3审查确认production没有Water资源、Scene组件、render feature、provider、query或buoyancy owner；现有PBR transmission、SSR、Planar Reflection、OIT、ApplyForce和Recast Navigation只是通用基础。`ocean.query.v1`仅为plugin bridge测试fixture，WOC water elemental也只验证普通glTF材质的IOR/transmission参数，均不能证明Water Body、wave、underwater或physics闭环。报告登记0项P0、62项P1、14项P2；既有产品阻断继续由Editor39、Runtime09G2和Runtime08A拥有，本篇要求建立body/zone、surface/LOD、reflection/refraction、wave/FFT/shallow simulation、query/buoyancy与44项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/30-water-ocean-lake-river-surface-wave-fft-shallow-water-rendering-underwater-buoyancy-query-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/30-water-ocean-lake-river-surface-wave-fft-shallow-water-rendering-underwater-buoyancy-query-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Cloth/Fabric/Soft Body运行时首轮E3审查确认production对`Cloth/SoftBody/Garment/Fabric`没有领域类型；Resource、Scene、Render Extract、render feature、Physics capability和Jolt bridge都没有布料实例、软体句柄、solver/provider或动态顶点输出。现有skin、Morph、Ragdoll、velocity与Standard PBR是真实底座，但GPU mesh只有静态vertex/index usage，材质也没有Fabric BRDF。报告登记0项P0、64项P1、14项P2；Runtime08A和Runtime09H1继续拥有既有占位与previous-deformation边界，本篇建立source/build artifact、sim/render topology mapping、constraint/collision/wind、generation-qualified deformation、Fabric shading、LOD/cache与36项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/31-cloth-fabric-soft-body-garment-simulation-collision-deformation-rendering-wind-lod-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/31-cloth-fabric-soft-body-garment-simulation-collision-deformation-rendering-wind-lod-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Hair/Groom/Fur/Strand运行时首轮E3审查确认production没有任何领域标识、资源、importer、Scene组件、binding、deformer/simulation、render feature、Hair visibility或Hair BSDF。现有triangle Mesh、Skin/Morph、generic OIT、ordinary shadow/velocity与anisotropic GGX是真实通用底座，但无法表达curve/group schema、root/guide interpolation、current/previous strand resources、coverage/deep transmittance、R/TT/TRT或representation LOD。报告登记0项P0、72项P1、16项P2；Runtime09F3继续拥有既有GI hair classification，本篇建立source/build/binding、runtime instance、simulation/cache、strands/cards/meshes、visibility/lighting/shadow/RT、streaming/scalability与40项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/32-hair-groom-fur-strand-source-binding-simulation-rendering-lighting-shadow-lod-streaming-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/32-hair-groom-fur-strand-source-binding-simulation-rendering-lighting-shadow-lod-streaming-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Vegetation/Tree/Foliage/Grass运行时首轮E3审查确认production没有species/prototype/instance-set/cluster、WindField、SpeedTree或Impostor；`Tree/Billboard/Terrain/MeshLod`只是无executor的advanced slot。`static_batches`没有graphics consumer，mesh产品路径固定单实例，LOD只看entity原点距离，shader没有vertex deformation hook；Vampire草地只是opaque double-sided预合并mesh。报告登记0项P0、72项P1、16项P2；Terrain29、Editor16、Runtime09B与App06继续拥有placement/authoring/generic GPU Scene/产品证据，本篇建立species/compiler、prototype/instance cluster、mesh-card-billboard-impostor LOD、wind/history、thin-leaf shading、streaming/scalability与40项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/34-vegetation-tree-foliage-grass-species-instancing-wind-animation-billboard-impostor-lod-streaming-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/34-vegetation-tree-foliage-grass-species-instancing-wind-animation-billboard-impostor-lod-streaming-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Decal运行时首轮E3审查确认production只登记`DecalComponent`描述符和一个位于PostProcess阶段的render pass；executor是显式`r`noop_render_executor`，frame extract、GPU Scene、material domain、receiver filter、DBuffer/GBuffer/forward path、atlas、culling、batching、temporal history、ray tracing与产品样例均没有闭环。报告登记0项新P0、72项运行时子P1、16项P2；Editor39继续拥有5项作者工具P0及P1-50..60父项，Plugins04继续拥有metadata/capability/package/product assembly真相，本篇补齐projection payload、prepared instance、receiver/write mask、technique selection、atlas residency、visibility/batching、temporal/RT和40项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/35-decal-projector-material-domain-dbuffer-gbuffer-forward-receiver-culling-batching-atlas-streaming-temporal-rt-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/35-decal-projector-material-domain-dbuffer-gbuffer-forward-receiver-culling-batching-atlas-streaming-temporal-rt-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Weather/Climate运行时首轮E3审查确认production没有package、source asset、compiler、artifact、World service、transition/region resolve、snapshot、Wind/Precipitation/Surface adapter或network/save闭环；Scene environment仍由`preview_skybox`布尔值生成，程序sun与DirectionalLight分裂，Particle CPU/GPU也没有共同Weather force。报告登记0项新P0、72项运行时子P1、16项P2；Editor38继续拥有5项父P0与P1-01..70跨域要求，本篇补齐program artifact、deterministic authority、celestial/region/network/save、typed domain adapters、scalability与40项资格门。详见[zircon_runtime/36-weather-climate-celestial-time-of-day-wind-precipitation-cloud-atmosphere-surface-state-determinism-network-save-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/36-weather-climate-celestial-time-of-day-wind-precipitation-cloud-atmosphere-surface-state-determinism-network-save-scalability-product-integration-review.md)。

Camera运行时首轮E3审查曾确认Scene asset/component到render descriptor、target/order/stack和per-camera history已有真实底座，并登记0项新P0、72项运行时子P1、16项P2与40项资格门；其“Scene不持久化active camera”描述已被Runtime126当前源码复核纠正为World v2保存裸EntityId，但stable generation identity仍缺失。历史冻结见[zircon_runtime/37-camera-endpoint-director-rig-controller-blend-shake-cinematic-cut-history-multiview-network-save-scalability-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/37-camera-endpoint-director-rig-controller-blend-shake-cinematic-cut-history-multiview-network-save-scalability-product-integration-review.md)，当前状态以[zircon_runtime/99za-runtime-camera-endpoint-director-rig-controller-blend-shake-cut-history-multiview-product-integration-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/99za-runtime-camera-endpoint-director-rig-controller-blend-shake-cut-history-multiview-product-integration-current-source-review.md)为准。

脚本入口横向审查确认2,210个tracked script-like文件、23个直接Python入口、49个非测试PowerShell入口、5个Node package的323条命令alias和3个workflow的33个shell block尚无统一CommandId、InterpreterSet、mutation/admission、CLI/exit或OperationReceipt。现有argparse、PowerShell strict mode、ShouldProcess/DryRun和lockfile可以保留，但process 0退出不能证明同代source、interpreter、artifact与terminal effect。详见[zircon_tooling/19-script-interpreter-entrypoint-command-registry-cli-operation-receipt-review.md](zircon_tooling/19-script-interpreter-entrypoint-command-registry-cli-operation-receipt-review.md)。

Cargo package图谱横向审查覆盖162份`Cargo.toml`、159个package、338个Cargo target和4套lock graph。Editor产品请求`target-editor-host`时，`zircon_editor`对`zircon_runtime`的默认依赖仍把`target-client`带入最终closure；现有CI只检查请求feature或手写profile数组，没有验证解析后的exact-one ProductRole。报告据此要求建立canonical package catalog、feature约束和可差分的ResolvedPackageGraphReceipt。详见[zircon_tooling/20-cargo-package-workspace-feature-dependency-target-graph-build-receipt-review.md](zircon_tooling/20-cargo-package-workspace-feature-dependency-target-graph-build-receipt-review.md)。

Unsafe Safety横向审查确认234个Rust文件出现1,656行unsafe相关文本；按路径排除明显测试后仍约有810个production-like unsafe block、73个production public/restricted-public unsafe函数和13个手工Send/Sync实现。必要的DLL、plugin、ECS、Jolt、Recast与ZrVM低层基础应保留，但只有WOC 4个crate默认forbid unsafe，全仓缺UnsafeUnitManifest、workspace lint、thread/module generation/unload合同与source-bound UnsafeEvidenceReceipt。具体P0仍由Plugin/Runtime Interface等原专项拥有，本轮没有重复计数。详见[zircon_tooling/21-unsafe-rust-ffi-native-memory-thread-affinity-panic-unload-safety-governance-review.md](zircon_tooling/21-unsafe-rust-ffi-native-memory-thread-affinity-panic-unload-safety-governance-review.md)。

Magic Constant与Policy横向审查对11,758个production-like Rust文件做保守inventory，确认7,224个命名const/static分布于1,848文件，其中1,791个policy-like定义分布于800文件；Editor只有6处SettingDefinition注册点和13个公开设置key，Runtime ConfigManager仍以String到serde_json::Value承载配置。IBL workgroup、Mesh SDF尺寸、DDS/KTX2布局、shadow face、IK pending、frame capture与frame demand等合同跨owner重复，usize/u64/Duration MAX又同时表达unlimited、invalid、missing和admission bypass。报告建立四级placement规则，以及ConstantUseInventory、PolicyDefinition Registry、ResolvedPolicySnapshot和source/build-bound evidence链；未把所有数字集中到单一常量文件，也未把机械字面量扫描当作缺陷计数。详见[zircon_tooling/22-magic-constant-sentinel-threshold-timeout-capacity-budget-policy-convergence-review.md](zircon_tooling/22-magic-constant-sentinel-threshold-timeout-capacity-budget-policy-convergence-review.md)。

Failure Contract横向审查对11,498个production-like Rust文件、约1,023,340行前缀代码做保守inventory。仓库已有451个typed Error定义、408处derive Error相关使用和74处catch_unwind，但24个panic、190个unreachable、950个expect、421处poison后继续与91处poison即终止候选没有共同failure domain；804个.ok、600个let _、1,061个unwrap_or_default和948个Result<_, String>候选又混合合法局部proof与真实错误丢失。报告要求以AST/Cargo-expanded SourceSet重取inventory，并建立FailureDomainDefinition、ErrorCode Registry、RecoveryDecision、TaskSupervisor与source/build-bound Failure/Crash Receipt；必要abort和strict API可保留，但必须声明soundness/precondition。详见[zircon_tooling/23-failure-contract-panic-unwind-error-propagation-poison-recovery-result-observability-review.md](zircon_tooling/23-failure-contract-panic-unwind-error-propagation-poison-recovery-result-observability-review.md)。

Concurrency横向审查对11,485个production-like Rust文件、约1,009,937行前缀代码做保守inventory。Runtime已有TaskPools/JobHandle、文本raster、diagnostic sink和pipelined render等可保留基础，但资产change subscriber仍用无界ongoing stream并在subscriber锁内wake，World inspection用六把RwLock分裂发布同一逻辑generation，network latency又用两个Relaxed atomic组成无happens-before的value/valid协议；产品也没有统一execution domain、线程拓扑预算、lock rank、blocking、shutdown或model-checking资格。报告建立ConcurrencySiteInventory、ExecutionDomain、ThreadTopology、LockGraph、AtomicInvariant、ChannelPolicy、TaskScope和ConcurrencyEvidenceReceipt链，不主张机械lock-free/SeqCst/async改写。详见[zircon_tooling/24-concurrency-locking-atomic-ordering-blocking-thread-lifecycle-backpressure-deadlock-review.md](zircon_tooling/24-concurrency-locking-atomic-ordering-blocking-thread-lifecycle-backpressure-deadlock-review.md)。

Security横向审查确认Script host-call capability、Rustls、Hub remote fail-close、Coordinator browser credential/CSRF/loopback/RBAC/audit、Tray secret Debug redaction和CI cargo-deny都是真实基础；但它们没有共同Principal/SecurityContext/CredentialLease/TrustReceipt/CapabilityDecision/SecurityAudit合同。Native DLL仍在trust验证前加载，plugin `.sig`只是可改写hash旁车，HTTP pinning会关闭标准证书验证，WebSocket pin不校验peer，TLS私钥与HTTP auth-bearing DTO又可Debug/Serialize，Hub CSP仍为null。报告建立O15跨产品Security Control Plane与BuildSet-bound资格链，具体修复继续路由Plugins01、Runtime07/08E、Hub03与Tooling09。详见[zircon_tooling/26-security-principal-credential-trust-capability-cryptography-supply-chain-audit-review.md](zircon_tooling/26-security-principal-credential-trust-capability-cryptography-supply-chain-audit-review.md)。

Version/Compatibility横向审查确认shared serialization、asset migration transaction、UI/VM migration和Coordinator数据库迁移是真实基础，但1,505处version field/498文件没有共同VersionDomain、VersionVector、SupportWindow、CompatibilityDecision或MigrationReceipt。Engine version仍借用crate package version，plugin native/runtime/tooling维护三套兼容解析，41个distribution统一自报ABI 3与`>=0.1,<0.2`却无BuildSet矩阵；Rust产品源码没有`#[deprecated]`，735处/123文件legacy信号也没有退出预算。报告建立O03跨产品版本域、迁移计划/凭证、升级降级与弃用资格链。详见[zircon_tooling/27-version-domain-schema-compatibility-support-window-migration-deprecation-upgrade-downgrade-review.md](zircon_tooling/27-version-domain-schema-compatibility-support-window-migration-deprecation-upgrade-downgrade-review.md)。

Memory横向审查对11,716个production-like Rust文件、约1,032,404行前缀代码做保守inventory。ECS连续列/heap estimate、4 MiB inline command arena、shaped-text capacity估算、asset/Editor/text/IO/foreign byte budget和局部allocation-free tests可保留，但artifact cache只统计map-owned Arc而不能约束external lease实际驻留，InlineCommandArena reset保留峰值capacity，UiSurfaceNodePool无entry/byte/age上限；CPU/GPU/mapped/foreign memory也没有统一domain、产品预算、pressure/reclamation、OOM和heap snapshot资格。报告建立AllocationSiteInventory、MemoryDomain/Tag、ProductMemoryBudget、MemoryReservation、SharedPayloadLease、Pressure Coordinator和MemoryEvidenceReceipt链，不主张未经benchmark替换allocator或机械shrink/pool化。详见[zircon_tooling/25-memory-allocation-domain-budget-oom-pressure-fragmentation-pooling-cache-residency-observability-review.md](zircon_tooling/25-memory-allocation-domain-budget-oom-pressure-fragmentation-pooling-cache-residency-observability-review.md)。

`zircon_plugins/neural`的41文件/7,016行逐文件审查已经完成：Editor默认能将ONNX导入为`r`neural.model`，但Runtime插件注册和Post Process extension为空、包外没有模型consumer；直接覆盖写盘、无界ONNX/`.znn`解析、缺失semantic graph admission、cook/cache、Model/ModelInstance与真实Render Graph执行形成5项P0和完整重构链。详见[zircon_plugins/02-neural-model-onnx-inference-post-process-editor-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/02-neural-model-onnx-inference-post-process-editor-product-integration-review.md)。

`editor_build_export_desktop`与`r`native_window_hosting`的25文件/2,039行逐文件审查也已完成：两包source registration未进入默认App catalog，dist均只发布空贡献；Desktop Export core产品与插件七个无factory operation/死profile-report形成双authority，Native Window capability则由core默认伪造且插件引用缺失ZUI。详见[zircon_plugins/03-desktop-export-native-window-source-dist-provider-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/03-desktop-export-native-window-source-dist-provider-integration-review.md)。

Rendering umbrella、15个feature bundle与Solari的180文件/9,506 LF行物理审查也已完成：普通Editor没有提交feature registration，catalog仍可从metadata发布available capability；generated export另行链接feature，preview/export product graph因此分叉。默认集合含空executor或无执行面的feature，Solari native dist声明的provider extension又被只重放systems的loader静默丢弃。详见[zircon_plugins/04-rendering-umbrella-feature-bundles-solari-native-provider-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/04-rendering-umbrella-feature-bundles-solari-native-provider-product-integration-review.md)。

新`shader_wgsl_importer`与旧`asset_importers/shader` family的14文件/1,469行逐文件审查也已完成：static catalog收录两包，linked provider catalog却均无依赖/分支，core反向直接拥有GLSL/SPIR-V；required capability不控制Function handler可用性，两份NativeDynamic没有import ABI，所有stage又固定为Surface并允许空reflection/layout报告ready。详见[zircon_plugins/05-shader-wgsl-family-importer-compiler-artifact-native-product-integration-review.md](zircon_plugins/05-shader-wgsl-family-importer-compiler-artifact-native-product-integration-review.md)。

首方插件catalog/profile/capability closure的39包/139 crate/162 target横向审查也已完成：runtime/editor manifest分别声明30/25个package，通用catalog只路由14/2，缺失16个runtime与23个editor source provider；Client、Editor与Dev profile把Sound/Rendering列为required，却没有链接base runtime catalog，missing required selection又被裸`Vec` API静默丢弃。详见[zircon_plugins/06-first-party-plugin-source-editor-runtime-dist-catalog-profile-capability-closure-review.md](zircon_plugins/06-first-party-plugin-source-editor-runtime-dist-catalog-profile-capability-closure-review.md)。

`examples/woc`多应用/多crate产品集成首轮E3审查也已完成：四个role binary都只打印identity后退出，仓内没有production `WocProjectVm`或WOC plugin registration；clean clone缺29个入口可达Zr module，native workspace又有6个compile error。native WOS83、package WOS113、writer118、reader117彼此冲突，transaction wrapper不能回滚已被`&mut self`修改的VM，全量64MiB state与16MiB JSON presentation热路径也不具备工程级性能基础。详见[zircon_app/03-woc-product-role-host-zrvm-transaction-state-client-server-integration-review.md](zircon_app/03-woc-product-role-host-zrvm-transaction-state-client-server-integration-review.md)。

WOC native client的61个production Rust文件也已逐文件完成E3审查：binary不引用library且只输出identity；manifest和source都没有window/GPU/UI/audio/network/async backend，关键window/settings/input model与host effect没有产品consumer。`ClientFrameDriver`还会在timeline失败后消费命令、推进movement并留下authority部分提交；online auth effect可Debug输出password/token/2FA且没有secure transport/request identity。详见[zircon_app/04-woc-native-client-window-input-shell-ui-presentation-frame-product-integration-review.md](zircon_app/04-woc-native-client-window-input-shell-ui-presentation-frame-product-integration-review.md)。

WOC native server/bot/headless的5个production Rust文件也已逐文件完成E3审查：三个binary都只打印identity，server binary不链接自己的fixed-tick library。driver在VM commit前破坏性清空queue，fault input只保留为可被覆盖的内存diagnostic；authority ingress没有principal/session/world binding，commit也没有把outcome、replication与durability绑定同一代际。详见[zircon_app/05-woc-native-server-bot-headless-service-tick-replication-persistence-operations-product-integration-review.md](zircon_app/05-woc-native-server-bot-headless-service-tick-replication-persistence-operations-product-integration-review.md)。

`examples/vampire`的173个tracked文件也已完成产品样例首轮E3审查：场景直接引用的4份模型源以及另3份同类模型源只存在于被ignore的本地状态，standard profile又不能闭合required provider，asset测试只能借test-only importer通过。唯一玩法脚本把移动和伤害放在variable update且不消费balance/behavior tree；10个real-VM测试全部ignore，主import test与当前WGSL静态漂移，README声明的两张accepted图不存在且性能基线跨代矛盾。详见[zircon_app/06-vampire-roguelite-example-project-asset-script-gameplay-evidence-product-integration-review.md](zircon_app/06-vampire-roguelite-example-project-asset-script-gameplay-evidence-product-integration-review.md)。

`templates/projects/renderable-empty`的17个文件及创建到证据链也已完成首轮E3审查：唯一template ID没有version、content digest或compatibility，生成项目不记录template/creator/BuildSet receipt，也不声明required provider。Editor创建会open并在post-commit failure回滚，Hub复制后即报告成功；默认Windows release export又在真实build/cook/install/run资格未闭合时对每个新项目可见。详见[zircon_app/07-renderable-empty-project-template-create-import-render-export-evidence-product-integration-review.md](zircon_app/07-renderable-empty-project-template-create-import-render-export-evidence-product-integration-review.md)。

App Product Host当前源码复审冻结120个production文件/16,628行、60个focused test文件/9,006行、23个跨crate consumer与19个五引擎参考文件。App01的4项P0仍全Open；扩展后的30项P1为26 Open、3 Partial、1 Closed，8项P2为7 Open、1 Closed。统一foreign-output预算、10 Hz失焦cadence、三owner terminal聚合和log shutdown退出码是真实进展；但server artifact、统一shutdown、multiwindow/lifecycle与typed Play IPC仍断开，native surface还只支持Win32，native plugin host可被`into_core`丢失，report写失败可覆盖primary cause。App08替代App01的currentness，不重复累计finding总数。详见[zircon_app/08-product-host-bootstrap-loop-dynamic-runtime-shutdown-current-source-review.md](zircon_app/08-product-host-bootstrap-loop-dynamic-runtime-shutdown-current-source-review.md)。

WOC ZrVM运行时内核首轮E3审查也已完成：`world/state.zr`以68,730行、534个公开字段承载全部world/domain/codec/test authority；`fixedTick`手写串行调用51个阶段，其中35个直接全实体扫描。普通tick又做完整decode/encode/digest及多重复制，entity退役不物理删除，decode还会补NPC和重算业务状态。详见[zircon_runtime/12-woc-zrvm-package-kernel-world-state-schedule-serialization-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/12-woc-zrvm-package-kernel-world-state-schedule-serialization-runtime-review.md)。

WOC战斗runtime首轮E3审查也已完成：`combat`有197个文件/21,357行，但109个非test-main模块中只有54个从产品root可达，55个不可达模块包含damage/death/threat/aura/effect sequence、Boss与词缀owner。产品仍以四套百级手写dispatch、77个HP直接写入点和copy-in/copy-out状态投影运行；308项known ability仅117项进入M4 generic projection，54个parity owner当前全部缺失。详见[zircon_runtime/13-woc-combat-casting-effect-aura-damage-threat-death-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/13-woc-combat-casting-effect-aura-damage-threat-death-runtime-review.md)。

WOC progression runtime首轮E3审查也已完成：`progression`有101个文件/12,849行，但55个非test-main模块只有15个进入普通fixed-tick执行图，40个断线模块包含item instance ledger、bank/trade/market、通用quest/craft与talent commit。产品库存仍只有item code/count平行数组，signed/rolled/enchant/bound/charges实例无法持久化；M5表只覆盖82 items与2 quests，16个相关parity owner全部缺失。详见[zircon_runtime/14-woc-progression-inventory-item-economy-crafting-quest-talent-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/14-woc-progression-inventory-item-economy-crafting-quest-talent-runtime-review.md)。

WOC social runtime首轮E3审查也已完成：`social`有60个文件/8,425行，31个非test-main模块只有10个进入产品图，21个断线模块包含完整chat、party/raid、arena/duel、Dungeon Finder、Fiesta/Yumi/Vale Cup owner。Catalog/client公开的SocialGraph、Dungeon Finder、Vale Cup及部分Duel/Arena命令没有reducer；Chat会消费并静默丢弃除ready外的合法消息，World又没有account principal、presence或disconnect/reconnect生命周期。可达Card Duel还存在`join`参数数量不一致、typed result被丢弃和每tick全量snapshot重建。详见[zircon_runtime/15-woc-social-identity-party-raid-chat-duel-arena-matchmaking-minigame-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/15-woc-social-identity-party-raid-chat-duel-arena-matchmaking-minigame-runtime-review.md)。

WOC instance runtime首轮E3审查也已完成：`instances`有103个文件/10,993行，57个非test-main模块的普通产品路径只到达4个规则叶，53个断线模块包含完整Dungeon/Delve/lockpick/reset与通用PetState。产品没有InstanceId、allocator、claim、transfer或isolated world，而以X/Z坐标分带选择静态collision layout；Catalog/client又公开14个无reducer命令，已路由pet仍含no-op/forced-false语义。46个test main只有39个有manifest，38个M7 binary目录和11个parity owner全部缺失。详见[zircon_runtime/16-woc-instance-dungeon-delve-pet-companion-lockout-reset-collision-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/16-woc-instance-dungeon-delve-pet-companion-lockout-reset-collision-runtime-review.md)。

WOC world runtime首轮E3审查也已完成：从208个world文件中剔除Runtime12的`state.zr`和Runtime16的instance helper后，仍有49个模块/15,575行；14个候选owner不在产品闭包。主移动每0.2单位采样，开放世界每次axis-return查询内部最坏约1,020次collider检查，caller取X/Z时约2,040次/段；16-yard grid仍全扫170项且没有进入主路由。`SpatialGridState`和`PathfindState`都只有测试consumer，后者在too-wide/unreachable时退回直线；spawn/interaction/corpse/world-boss owner也断线。74个world-entry package有64个binary目录缺失，六个current parity owner不存在。详见[zircon_runtime/17-woc-world-terrain-collision-locomotion-spawn-spatial-targeting-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/17-woc-world-terrain-collision-locomotion-spawn-spatial-targeting-runtime-review.md)。

WOC generated content runtime首轮E3审查也已完成：`src/generated`有102文件/81,093行/3,362,552 bytes，连同`src/content`形成104个非测试主体；产品根只到达46个generated文件，三个content facade与另外55个generated候选断线。生成代码把内容编成约58K个`if`和3,382个`throw`，七大表合计48,466行；产品schema又未聚合21份局部digest，进程同时消费308项known ability、117项M4能力、162个talent options、189个modifiers、55个procs及M3/M5/M8分期表而没有唯一ContentBuildSet。M4/M5测试固定的SHA和21/14计数已被当前117/82项产物静态推翻。详见[zircon_runtime/18-woc-generated-content-catalog-buildset-install-query-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/18-woc-generated-content-catalog-buildset-install-query-runtime-review.md)。

WOC command protocol runtime首轮E3审查也已完成：current-head有165个command但typed payload contract仅157项，8个dispatch-only命令未映射；native validator虽覆盖61类payload，`Command`/`FixedTickInput` decode却不调用它，脚本只做kind/length检查后仍由68K行WorldState手工解析。movement relay会以旧或重复sequence覆盖较新flags，frame又不绑定完整catalog/payload/world/content identity；10个native required source被ignore，Rust/npm/Zr证据链均阻断。详见[zircon_runtime/19-woc-command-protocol-payload-codec-admission-movement-outcome-runtime-review.md](zircon_runtime/19-woc-command-protocol-payload-codec-admission-movement-outcome-runtime-review.md)。

WOC package root与World API首轮E3审查也已完成：reference catalog声明28个facet/248个member，但28个`woc_owner`中只有Card Minigame一个文件存在，且该文件与catalog要求的5个公开成员0/5匹配。Rust inventory validator忽略signature、owner path与member_count，不验证实现或artifact；唯一projection还接受任意pid与mutable service读取私有手牌。normal package不注册该API，Card test target缺产物，根artifact manifest又漏module并引用12个不存在文件。详见[zircon_runtime/20-woc-package-root-world-api-facet-registry-snapshot-command-publication-review.md](zircon_runtime/20-woc-package-root-world-api-facet-registry-snapshot-command-publication-review.md)。

WOC parity/oracle/evidence首轮E3审查也已完成：固定commit的54份golden与1,070项trace symbol静态校验通过，但native唯一double-run两次复制expected，54个catalog actual owner全部不存在，四条M3 trace又以1,039-1,756个数字字面量手写expected bytes而非采样产品world。materializer从固定commit取golden，reference probe却执行可变工作树；本轮`entity_roster`动态probe因身份漂移直接失败。详见[zircon_tooling/11-woc-parity-oracle-trace-golden-differential-replay-evidence-review.md](zircon_tooling/11-woc-parity-oracle-trace-golden-differential-replay-evidence-review.md)。

顶层acceptance archive与serialization fixture corpus首轮E3审查也已完成：100篇Markdown已被policy定义为历史归档，却只有41篇frontmatter、25篇header status、0篇source/build identity和0篇machine result link；29篇保留372次Windows绝对路径，14篇有46次精确失效路径。仓内仍有两处test直接读取历史Markdown字符串。6个共享serialization fixture都有真实consumer且静态解析成功，但没有原始writer/build、immutable digest或corpus manifest。详见[zircon_tooling/12-top-level-acceptance-archive-serialization-fixture-provenance-currentness-migration-review.md](zircon_tooling/12-top-level-acceptance-archive-serialization-fixture-provenance-currentness-migration-review.md)。

Editor Workbench DesignSpec、截图导出、视觉证据与prototype控制面首轮E3审查也已完成：16个tool文件共15,483行，驱动270项design与271张tracked PNG；当前标准verifier因clean tracked fixture digest漂移exit 1且没有required CI。Evidence把固定design未请求的4个fixture/31个icon列为依赖，却遗漏真正使用fixture的legacy app；完整导出又同时写candidate与其自认证hash，没有approved/diff/reviewer/promotion。270页浏览器扫描只有140种DOM结构，192个editor-page也没有capability owner或真实产品验收映射。详见[zircon_tooling/14-editor-workbench-design-spec-screenshot-export-visual-evidence-prototype-governance-review.md](zircon_tooling/14-editor-workbench-design-spec-screenshot-export-visual-evidence-prototype-governance-review.md)。

MVP BuildSet、产品进程、验收证据与resource baseline控制面首轮E3审查也已完成：33个tool文件共14,789行，17个直接测试约9,012行；底层Windows no-follow handle、snapshot/projection和原子发布值得保留，但上层从可变worktree构建且fingerprint吞入2,373个untracked文件/1.68 GB，ProductReceipt不绑定真实toolchain/runtime dependencies。runtime/editor可用自报marker和任意非空PNG自认证，resource reporter在没有任何产品observation producer时仍能把caller JSON标为`measured`；14个focused文件当前81/84通过，required workflow又不运行这些控制面suite。详见[zircon_tooling/15-mvp-build-staging-product-process-acceptance-evidence-resource-baseline-control-plane-review.md](zircon_tooling/15-mvp-build-staging-product-process-acceptance-evidence-resource-baseline-control-plane-review.md)。

跨域capability真相、placeholder/no-op与fallback/degraded资格控制面首轮审查也已完成：生产owner没有可执行TODO，三个`unimplemented!`都在测试替身；真正缺口是manifest/config/registration membership可以越级投影为`Complete/Available`，默认成功生命周期、render no-op和Play no-op又没有统一semantic-effect约束，数百个局部fallback状态也不进入current product qualification。报告建立Declared→Loaded→Ready→Executed→Qualified分层、ProviderInstance/ExecutionObservation/FallbackEvent/QualificationReceipt及发布预算路线；真实功能实现仍由各domain专项拥有。详见[zircon_tooling/16-capability-truth-placeholder-noop-fallback-degraded-qualification-control-plane-review.md](zircon_tooling/16-capability-truth-placeholder-noop-fallback-degraded-qualification-control-plane-review.md)。

Repository Content、SourceSet、ignore、checked-in generated/vendor与license分发完整性首轮审查也已完成：27,432个tracked path约1.335 GB，其中2,849个/93.9 MB同时被当前ignore命中；MVP与Coordinator都用`--exclude-standard`枚举新增source，因此WOC/Vampire/`.codex`/`.txt`宽规则可让required新文件逃逸fingerprint。159个Cargo包声明MIT OR Apache-2.0但Git tree只有MIT文本，WOC声称已materialize的source license也只存在于本机ignored状态；根又没有ContentClass、NoticeGraph或可验证SourceArchive。详见[zircon_tooling/17-repository-content-source-set-ignore-generated-vendor-license-distribution-review.md](zircon_tooling/17-repository-content-source-set-ignore-generated-vendor-license-distribution-review.md)。

Executable Target、Entrypoint、CLI与Process Receipt横向审查也已完成：仓库18个真实Rust binary由8个产品和10个工具/生成器/控制程序组成，12个显式`[[bin]]`、6个依赖Cargo自动发现，只有3个manifest关闭`autobins`。当前没有统一TargetId/role/variant/artifact/install/launch/qualification contract，CLI又混用JSON、自由文本、Rust `Result`、panic及1/2/3/4退出码；构建、spawn或0退出不能证明正确代次进入Ready并执行了目标能力。详见[zircon_tooling/18-executable-target-entrypoint-cli-process-receipt-product-qualification-review.md](zircon_tooling/18-executable-target-entrypoint-cli-process-receipt-product-qualification-review.md)。

补充进度：`zircon_runtime_interface` 已完成 UI authoring/accessibility/input/diagnostic/status第三轮、profiling/plugin-event/script-diagnostic/native manifest收口第四轮、`zircon_runtime_host`全量safe/unsafe ownership、admission/fuse、budget/output family/observability第五轮、Project Manifest/Session Lock/Hub Protocol/Focus/Recent跨进程合同第六轮，以及Contract Certification/ABI Layout/Version Skew/Cross-Language/Fuzz第七轮E3静态审查；Interface15又对该认证链完成第十五轮当前源码复核。第六轮保留partial parser、UUID token、OS lease、原子发布和strict marker基础，但确认partial probe、lease、Ready、focus publish与recent write被混成错误产品承诺；新增0项P0、56项P1和14项P2，Editor51五项项目生命周期P0保持原owner。第七轮确认现有401项crate测试不能替代真实DLL、跨语言、历史版本、corpus/fuzz和故障隔离资格，新增1项P0、48项P1和12项P2；Interface15重判这些旧条目但不重复计数。详见 [zircon_runtime_interface/03-ui-authoring-accessibility-input-diagnostic-status-public-contract-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/03-ui-authoring-accessibility-input-diagnostic-status-public-contract-review.md)、[zircon_runtime_interface/04-profiling-plugin-event-script-diagnostic-manifest-crate-ownership-consolidation-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/04-profiling-plugin-event-script-diagnostic-manifest-crate-ownership-consolidation-review.md)、[zircon_runtime_interface/05-runtime-host-foreign-output-safe-api-ownership-admission-budget-fuse-observability-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/05-runtime-host-foreign-output-safe-api-ownership-admission-budget-fuse-observability-review.md)、[zircon_runtime_interface/06-project-manifest-session-lock-hub-protocol-recent-project-cross-process-contract-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/06-project-manifest-session-lock-hub-protocol-recent-project-cross-process-contract-product-integration-review.md) 与 [zircon_runtime_interface/07-contract-certification-abi-layout-version-skew-cross-language-fuzz-test-architecture-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/07-contract-certification-abi-layout-version-skew-cross-language-fuzz-test-architecture-review.md)。

Runtime DLL ABI/FFI第八轮当前源码复核确认V7 opaque allocation、checked slices、共享payload/frame budgets与session handle exhaustion已形成真实底座，但legacy `ZrOwnedByteBuffer`、BuildSet/capability/InterfaceSpec、bounded quiesce、真实DLL/cross-language资格仍未闭合；Runtime的destroy-release-retry策略与App首次失败即abort互相冲突，Editor gateway也存在table size与空frame语义裂缝。Interface01原43项现为27 Open、14 Partial、2 Closed，新增3项P1 Open，21项Gate为15 Fail、5 Partial、1 Pass。详见[zircon_runtime_interface/08-runtime-dll-abi-ffi-version-handle-foreign-ownership-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/08-runtime-dll-abi-ffi-version-handle-foreign-ownership-current-source-review.md)。

Runtime Host foreign output第九轮当前源码复核确认V7 opaque allocation、六类共享policy、per-session fuse、基础metrics与allocation-free JSON syntax preflight是可保留底座；但safe `decode_json`/status路径仍可解引用Safe Rust伪造裸指针，`Copy` releaser未绑定provider/session epoch，`ensure_* -> FFI`仍有admission race。新preflight又以encoded bytes代理JSON节点并固定双重解析，未覆盖key、typed item和decoded heap。Interface05原91项现为85 Open、5 Partial、1 Closed，新增1项P1 Open，18项Gate为11 Fail、6 Partial、1 Pass。详见[zircon_runtime_interface/09-runtime-host-foreign-output-safe-owner-admission-budget-fuse-observability-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/09-runtime-host-foreign-output-safe-owner-admission-budget-fuse-observability-current-source-review.md)。

UI authoring/accessibility/input/diagnostic/status/operation第十二轮当前源码复核确认v2 graph validator/compiled fingerprint、surface分域generation、bounded accessibility producer、input effect transaction、operation service与Host metrics可保留；但lossless authoring、observed-generation accessibility action、typed control/operation、bounded subscription/debug、Host side-effect receipt与App/Editor accessibility consumer仍未成立。Interface03旧3项P0、72项P1、12项P2当前分别为1 Open/2 Partial、60 Open/12 Partial、10 Open/2 Partial；32门为19 Fail、13 Partial、0 Pass，不新增唯一总账。详见[zircon_runtime_interface/12-ui-authoring-accessibility-input-diagnostic-status-operation-public-contract-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime_interface/12-ui-authoring-accessibility-input-diagnostic-status-operation-public-contract-current-source-review.md)。

Editor Terrain/Landscape、Foliage/Scatter与World Partition/Level Streaming Authoring也已完成首轮E3静态审查；它确认Terrain资产/插件描述符基础存在，但backend、可逆编辑、植被散布、partition manifest和runtime streaming尚未形成产品闭环。Editor Sound/Audio Clip/Mixer/Spatial/Acoustic/Timeline Authoring亦完成首轮纵向审查；它保留真实Runtime Sound service、codec导入与live-output controller基础，同时确认默认资产入口、operation factory、产品controller、真实surface、transaction/audition和scene bridge没有闭环。Editor Physics Material/RigidBody/Collider/Joint/Collision Cook/Ragdoll/Debug Authoring也完成首轮纵向审查；它保留Runtime scene/Jolt、材料资产、overlay builder和ragdoll generator基础，同时确认默认入口、surface、cook、可逆编辑、ragdoll asset/preview和viewport provider没有闭环。Editor Navigation Settings/NavMesh/Agent/Area/Surface/Modifier/Obstacle/Off-mesh Link/Bake/Query/Debug Authoring也完成首轮纵向审查；它保留默认插件装配、Recast/Detour、Runtime manager、operation snapshot与PIE provider基础，同时确认Bake生产实现和test矛盾、asset/component视图占位、异步job/transaction/query/toggle以及静态Workbench均未闭环。Runtime AI审查则确认typed tree/Blackboard/owner lease与分帧Perception基础存在，但普通scene无法启动agent，三个标准节点没有名称承诺的副作用，MoveTo/latent abort不闭合，Sight在Physics缺失或失败时默认穿墙，camera LOD与无读者全量debug又会改变业务时序并放大热路径成本。

Editor AI Behavior Tree/Blackboard/Perception/EQS/Debug Authoring也已完成首轮E3静态审查；它保留typed event、session fence、World-qualified mirror与owner-aware extension基础，同时确认AI Editor current source静态编译不兼容、默认first-party Editor catalog没有AI、5个可见operation无factory、graph/surface/mirror/provider没有产品consumer，并且默认Workbench用固定BT、agent和成功反馈构成第二套假authority。

Runtime Gameplay Ability/Effect/Attribute/Tag/Cue/Prediction审查确认当前没有Ability System：唯一 `zr.zircon.gameplay` 是通用脚本entity host，`damage_entity`仍通过 `script.bindings`中的字符串 `hp`直接改值并可despawn实体，一个粗粒度 `gameplay.entity` capability又覆盖任意实体transform/component/spawn/despawn/damage/heal。与此同时Workbench已公开Effect/Ability/Tags、Server Initiated和predicted activation，但runtime没有Tag/Attribute/Effect/Ability/Cue artifact、scene/world owner、authority、prediction、rollback或replication provider。

Editor Gameplay Ability/Effect/Attribute/Tag/Cue/Debug Authoring审查确认首屏三份ZUI虽有90个control和68条event/route，但没有Gameplay Editor provider或真实asset/document。字段提交只改retained control字符串，Save/Compile/Diff/Apply/Playtest/Add/Rename与九类bottom panel只投影固定结果；必须先硬切Unavailable，再以Runtime08G共享schema/compiler、transaction、sandbox/PIE/network trace和Tag migration取代第二套假authority。

Editor Render Pipeline/Frame Capture/Lighting Bake/Reflection Probe/Post Process/Debug Authoring审查确认Runtime已有Render Graph report、GPU timing/capture与Reflection Probe真实capture/register helper，但默认Editor catalog没有Rendering，根与15个feature Editor descriptor总计只有16个capability贡献。Render Workbench固定Frame 1234、DX12与6.24 ms，Lighting Bake固定87 lightmaps/02:30，Post Process固定Global Stack/Filmic/LUT，所有Compile/Bake/Apply只写字符串；必须先撤销`stable/complete`与静态成功，再接入generation-qualified runtime provider、真实job/artifact和transactional profile/volume。

Editor UI Asset/HUD/Widget/Binding/Theme/Icon/Accessibility/Menu Flow/Font Atlas Authoring审查确认真实UI Asset Editor已有typed session、V1/V2 preview、tree/palette、style/theme/binding、watcher/conflict/recovery基础，但V2视觉保存会删除`repeat`/node `slots`并把`ThemeTokens`降级，Save在磁盘写入前清dirty，普通Save可覆盖冲突，多文件promote/undo会部分提交，默认create插件又引用四份不存在资源且无operation factory。七份UI Workbench仍以固定HUD、binding、icon、a11y、menu和font数据构成第二authority；必须先完成lossless document、revision CAS、跨资产transaction与真实factory，再接入Runtime11A/11B/11C证据。

Editor Spawn Rules/Encounter/Population/World State/Scenario/Quest Flag/Authority/Simulation Authoring审查确认ECS与DynamicScene已有真实结构事务底座，但两张Gameplay Workbench固定伪造18 rules/12 zones/96 spawns、84 keys/6 layers/42 events和Server authority；仓内没有对应生产domain，DynamicScene又只返回无owner/lifecycle的`EntityRemap`。必须建立stable Spawn Definition/Compiled Plan/Runtime Instance与typed World State/Scenario共享编译链，并把Editor simulation接入隔离World及真实多人role。

Editor Input Action/Mapping Context/Binding/Trigger/Modifier/Device/User/Rebinding/Accessibility Authoring审查确认Runtime evaluator已有compiled generation和10K规模基础，但Editor没有Input asset/toolkit，production Action Manager固定从disabled空map构造，可序列化binding又硬编码当前`GamepadId`。必须建立source/compiler/artifact、per-user device assignment/profile/rebind和action snapshot，并迁移脚本raw `key_pressed`旁路。

Editor Camera Asset/Component/Rig/Controller/Director/Blend/Shake/Cinematic Cut/Preview Authoring审查确认Scene camera source、render descriptor/stack validator和三类controller数学基础真实存在，但CameraComponent 14字段仅3字段可编辑，Scene无法创作render stack，动态Runtime又无条件让右键/中键/滚轮直接改global active camera。全仓没有production Rig/Director/Blend/Shake/Cine产品，Sequencer Camera Cut也没有typed track或history事件；必须建立endpoint + Rig/Lens/Shake compiler、per-player/per-viewport director和cut/history epoch闭环。

Editor Script Source/Code Editor/Build/Compiler/Hot Reload/Debugger/Visual Script/Class/Component Authoring审查确认Runtime已有bounded package discovery、真实ZrVM compile/session、slot generation和可回滚state/reflection migration，外部ZrVM也已有LSP/debug能力；但required provider未进入默认Client/Editor feature组，Editor build状态机没有production caller，Runtime startup又同步另编一次。Editor没有Script source/LSP/真实行列跳转，Scene binding仍是动态JSON map，也没有Visual Script或VM debugger产品；必须统一workspace/source/artifact/install generation、typed Class/Component schema和文本/图脚本debug链。

Editor Model/Mesh/Skeleton/Geometry Import/LOD/Collision/Retarget/Preview Authoring审查确认Mesh attribute/morph、meshopt/quantization/material extension、labeled subasset、Mesh SDF/Virtual Geometry和CPU/GPU skinning底座真实存在；但Quick Import路径框没有生产event，Core与split glTF输出语义不等价且高priority插件可覆盖核心，referenced-Mesh Model overview仍只读空inline payload，Renderer不消费导入IBM且Scene不安装Skeleton/player。Model/Mesh/Skeleton没有toolkit或真实thumbnail，LOD只读且按origin距离选择，Collision/Retarget/VG产品面又是固定反馈或缺失模板；必须建立versioned recipe、normalized scene、独立Skin/Skeleton binding、derived artifact和共享Preview Scene。

Editor Localization/String Table/Culture/Translation Import-Export/Fallback/Pseudo-localization/Preview Authoring审查确认Editor shell已有两份54-key对齐catalog、settings热切换、bounded locale event/resync与notification locale snapshot，Runtime font culture/shaping和UI dependency collector也是真实基础；但`text_key`只靠fabricated empty String通过校验，renderer不解析localized table，manifest无cook/runtime consumer。UI Asset locale preview固定三项且只做key存在性报告，项目又没有typed String Table/Target/Archive、culture authority、gather/import/export/compile、script API或shipping chunk；必须建立共享identity、deterministic pipeline、generation-qualified service和真实translated/pseudo/RTL preview。

Editor Sprite/Atlas/TileSet/TileMap/Canvas 2D/Animation/Collision/Preview Authoring审查确认Runtime已有可工作的Sprite component/extract/renderer、Fit/Fill/Tiled/Sliced和严格Atlas校验，TileSet/TileMap也进入typed asset/load/reference基础；但Scene会在标准load/save删除TileMap且无法持久化Sprite2D，Tilemap render feature只有enum/slot，插件五个operation无factory且两份ZUI不存在。Atlas packer只服务Editor UI cache且无production caller/atomic artifact，TileSet/TileMap又只有单图、单tileset、dense numeric cell和string collider；必须先建立stable identity、schema v2/migration、source/recipe/artifact、Scene/Runtime chunk/cook与transactional toolkits。

Runtime SaveGame/Checkpoint/Slot/Participant/Migration/Platform/Cloud审查确认565文件Session archive、Dynamic Scene preflight/commit、versioned serialization、preferences与durable resource IO是真实基础；但capture/restore字段条件不对称，archive外层version 1无迁移，writer无fsync/startup recovery且CAS仅在进程内。WOC/Vampire仍是hot-reload或常量fixture，仓内没有player/profile/platform-user service、cloud provider或产品caller；必须严格区分玩家SaveGame、server checkpoint、network snapshot和hot-reload state，按Editor24、Runtime05/12与App03/05/06父owner关闭真实闭环。

Runtime Operation Service审查确认session级registry、task/retained-byte容量、owner snapshot/worker prepare/owner apply、deadline/cancel和两阶段harvest是真实基础；但请求没有descriptor/schema/identity/priority/idempotency，HashMap选择无序，cancel/deadline不停止worker，progress固定0/1，submit/frame demand不触发reactive wake，ABI无cancel/deadline/subscribe且shutdown不排空。导航Bake固定失败与Editor 16次poll继续由Editor19/Runtime08D拥有；共享服务按Runtime02、Runtime24、Interface01/05、App01和Tooling37父合同重构。

Runtime Builtin Module/Catalog/Profile/Target/Feature/Extension Assembly审查确认typed preset、依赖闭包、availability分类、拓扑排序与结构化load report是真实基础；但project selection无法约束registration，同feature多provider会全部注入，required capability不参与admission，`#[cfg]`改变Profile/schema，Runtime catalog、builtin assembly与App PluginGroup又形成四套composition truth。必须按Plugins06/01、App01与Runtime01/07/21父合同收敛为单一immutable composition plan、transactional activation和final receipt。

## 当前最高风险

- [Plugins22 Desktop Export / Native Window当前源码复核](zircon_plugins/22-desktop-export-native-window-source-dist-provider-current-source-review.md)确认两项静态修正已落地，但两包仍未进入first-party Editor catalog，两份dist仍序列化空贡献且可显示Loaded/Enabled，Native capability仍由core缺省配置伪造；Desktop同时存在插件7个死operation、wizard pipeline和retained job queue三套事实源。Plugins03的62项重判为56 Open、0 Partial、6 Closed。

- 普通Update/PostUpdate消费raw real delta而绕过Virtual pause、time scale和最大delta，后台/恢复长帧可直接进入Animation、script与游戏逻辑。
- Fixed clock在执行前预推进整批steps，每个子步读取相同最终tick/elapsed；第N步失败时仍宣称全部时间已提交，无法形成可信snapshot、RNG、network或replay边界。
- CPU World translation仍是absolute f32，GPU Scene/SceneUniform继续上传absolute f32 current/previous矩阵与camera；没有origin generation、rebase transaction或render-relative history correction，当前不能支撑可验证large world。
- Zr real backend依赖工作区外未固定且当前dirty的`zr_vm`仓库和本机动态库；Cargo.lock与required CI都不能重建同一compiler/VM。
- `.zro` loader不验证signature、major/minor、native width、instruction width或endian，也没有统一count/depth/bytes budget与pre-execution verifier。
- Zr incremental key只覆盖FNV source/zro hash和imports，artifact逐个覆盖final path；tracked manifest含绝对路径、14个缺失output和31个未索引`.zro`。

- WOC默认check只覆盖28/386个tracked脚本且本轮在第7步因157/148合同计数漂移失败；198个脚本无任一package入口，`.github`又没有WOC lane。
- WOC 130个脚本直接写产物、0个使用rename，64个generator发布多输出但没有generation/transaction/journal；Rust、ZrVM与JSON可形成mixed generation。
- 106个generated ZrVM文件含56,242个`if`并在解释模式运行；内容数据被迫编译成控制流，rank/effect选择还越过generated leaf-data边界。
- `zircon_app/build.rs`用build-host `cfg!`决定target linker arg；Editor build script又以raw TOML维护不完整plugin catalog authority。
- Session Coordinator的legacy与control入口均无真实认证：runtime token固定为空，所有本机请求被映射为`maintainer`，既有cookie/CSRF/elevation/role体系被整体旁路。
- 同一无认证`/command`可提交任意validation argv并直接Popen；调用方还能自行把ticket写成passed，integration candidate又不校验ticket与候选blob/input tree的绑定。
- milestone finalize在live worktree验证、用shared index tree提交，并在长验证前后snapshot/restore用户index；既不能证明commit字节通过，也可能抹掉外部新暂存内容。
- 普通validation没有Job Object、creation identity、deadline或restart reattach；daemon重启后活PID可永久running且无法取消/收集，子进程与继承pipe也可阻塞终态。
- 仓库外参考代码除外的161个Cargo manifest没有常规benchmark target/framework；634个性能finding仍无accepted baseline、统计趋势、硬件归一化或merge/release gate，不能支持“优于Unreal”的可审计声明。
- UI profile把未校验scenario拼进多处输出路径，可用`..`逃逸profile和tracked screenshot根；PBR writer/capture要求schema 2 managed provenance，最终summarizer却只接受schema 1 local provenance，合法流程必然失败。
- 产品只有两个Rust binary安装“先flush旧日志再委托默认hook”的panic hook；没有native minidump、线程/模块上下文、Crash Reporter、build/debug ID symbol store或自动symbolication。
- `docs/tests`已跟踪约823 MiB证据，其中37个RDC约632 MiB、651个PNG约119 MiB；没有LFS/CAS、root catalog、retention/currentness和accepted baseline promotion合同。
- Shader variant disk key只覆盖canonical variant与include hash，不覆盖WGSL、template或Naga/WGPU版本；prewarm又按该不足key去重并为第二个不同source虚报written coverage。
- Shader WGSL/meta使用固定temp双文件独立rename；并发writer可互相覆盖，rename失败时只要目标存在便返回成功，mixed generation或他人内容可被当成本次成功。
- Asset chunk CAS、UI compiled pair、IBL blob、shader pair与Cargo cache分属五套局部协议；没有统一Action Digest、Record/Value、local/remote policy、disk GC/quota、determinism verifier或cache receipt。
- 非参考源码未发现产品级remote executor；Coordinator仍是自由argv与非hermetic workspace，且既有token-free控制面在认证完成前不能扩展成remote worker/cache入口。
- 仓库没有Git tag、release/publish workflow、可信Release Manifest、候选promotion ledger或不可变产品artifact repository；Hub本地source build退出0便显示staged payload。
- NativeDynamic的`.sig`只是可共同篡改的hash/audit TOML且没有consumer；Hub NSIS、engine distribution和update metadata均无可轮换签名信任根。
- Hub没有updater provider，项目device install只复制到新目录；整个产品没有version slot、atomic current pointer、durable update journal、启动health gate或自动rollback。
- [Tooling Test Architecture 10](zircon_tooling/10-test-architecture-partition-selection-isolation-fixture-flake-results-review.md)确认Hub manifest显式`test = false`，导致源码中的258项inline单元测试不进入默认workspace test；39个外部测试偏源码字符串形状，不能替代行为测试。
- 主CI只显式执行3个Python模块，而Tool Python测试有659个文件；36个PowerShell tests、Coordinator Web check、Hub前端测试和WOC完整check均没有统一required lane。
- Cargo、Python、PowerShell、Node和MVP结果没有source/build-bound TestPlan/Attempt/ValidationSet；局部绿色、零测试或旧结果无法形成可证明完整的release admission。
- PBR viewer运行期致命错误只退出event loop，进程仍可能返回0；后台scene线程无cancel/join，关闭时不能确定性drain。
- managed截图与RenderDoc证据固定走offscreen CPU readback并绕过ProductHost/native swapchain；ready图像只检查非空颜色，没有golden/HDR/perceptual correctness oracle。
- `docs/tests/runtime/shader`已跟踪约595 MiB历史artifact，其中17个RDC约440 MiB；缺少内容寻址存储、retention/currentness和current-source自动回归合同。
- Runtime DLL的owned byte carrier为`Clone + Copy`，释放端按调用方可篡改的pointer/len/capacity和固定token重建`Vec`，存在双重释放、伪造释放与allocator corruption风险。
- V6加载只比较版本和结构大小，没有Build Set ID、target/data model、feature/schema fingerprint；它既不能证明内部DLL锁步身份，也没有公开ABI的版本/能力协商。
- 借用slice和status diagnostics可在统一形状/预算检查前构造Rust slice；frame dimensions与多类JSON输入输出又没有producer-side hard budget。
- Native plugin SDK对任意`T`无条件实现`Sync`，owned buffer可复制并按可伪造token重建`Vec`，borrowed slice又能制造任意生命周期。
- Editor/App在project selection之前先执行目录内所有editor native candidate；disabled/unselected不能阻止DLL initializer和entry运行，构建侧hash/signature也未进入loader准入。
- 39个第一方dist全部没有command/bridge/host-ready行为，业务native projection又全部`systems: []`；当前`r`native_dynamic`主要是metadata shell，不等价于同名静态插件。
- 生成manifest宣称SDK API 0.2.0，但runtime/editor source descriptor仍可发布默认0.1.0；loader只校验格式和engine range，没有SDK compatibility policy。
- Editor单tab关闭和`File > Close Project`都会绕过统一dirty gate并删除session/toolkit；window close prompt虽枚举dirty document，但Save分支明确未实现。
- UI asset与animation权威保存直接`fs::write`覆盖源文件；crash residual lock又只会阻止工程打开，既有takeover/autosave/restore基础没有产品入口。
- Core Save All、generation token、autosave与transaction基础已存在，但产品仍分裂为Global history、UI asset私有无界undo栈、animation局部dirty bool和多套保存协议。
- Editor打开非default scene后仍把当前world保存到manifest default scene；打开另一scene又会先替换world并清空history，不经过dirty决策。
- Scene DTO的Prefab instance在World load时未消费、save时固定写`None`；Prefab Tools五个可见operation均无factory，runtime importer也只提供backend-not-installed诊断。
- Multi-selection只在模型层成立，highlight/gizmo/frame/overlay仍只看primary；子节点gizmo又把local transform当world transform，父级旋转/缩放下会错误编辑。
- Editor同时维护Runtime registry、未接入产品的EditorAssetIndex和实际Asset Browser catalog三套资产视图；配套ImportFlow没有production caller，成功测试也明确停在Stale。
- UI/animation/model/scene等真实入口仍同步直调Runtime importer，多个保存路径吞掉导入错误；所谓catalog增量仍复制全量source/map并重建所有details/reference/folder projection。
- Plugin asset importer descriptor只有extension/operation查询，没有Runtime执行与settings schema绑定；缩略图也只有source image/placeholder，Operation provider在产品路径无法分派。
- Inspector把primary的Name、Parent、Local Transform及所有可编辑插件属性无dirty mask地写到全部selected node；现有测试还把这种整对象覆盖固化为成功语义。
- Inspector用两位小数字符串保存Translation/Scale draft，任何无关Apply都会把显示精度写回authoritative world；built-in Camera/Mesh/Light/Physics等reflection registration又完全没有进入通用Inspector。
- Field editor的Boolean/Color/Enum/Asset/Curve kind、asset markers以及custom surface controller/data root/bindings在host projection前丢失；production只剩NumberField/InputField，custom layout build没有调用。
- Editor插件启停先改变capability/lifecycle并发布状态，随后才保存project manifest；保存失败没有回滚或durable repair state，本次会话与重启后的启用结果可相反。
- Native Unload/Hot Reload已接真实host，却不发布新plugin status generation；pane按旧Arc命中缓存，成功卸载后仍可能显示`enabled | loaded`，watcher结果又只写`stderr`。
- 主Plugin Manager绕过core的generation-bound lifecycle panel source，另用manifest布尔值和diagnostic字符串推断状态；settings contribution在catalog merge被漏掉，静态showcase还以固定数据模拟完整管理器。
- 项目A在Play时可直接Close并打开B；`clear_project/replace_world`都保留A的`EditorPlaySession`，A子进程late exit随后会把A scene覆盖到当前B world，形成跨项目authoring数据破坏。
- Play backend在终止确认前`take()`唯一child owner；stop失败后无法可靠重试，自然退出后的plugin恢复失败又让controller保持Playing，而空backend继续报告Running，runtime事实与控制面可永久分裂。
- `UiControlRequest::InvokeBinding/InvokeRoute`没有复用`CallAction`的remote-callable gate；operation binding还把来源改写成UiBinding/RetainedHost，远程策略与审计provenance同时失真。
- Plugin SDK和共享DTO接受二段command ID，Editor宿主强制至少三段；serialized plugin Command即使修复ID也只注册operation descriptor而没有factory，公开贡献合同不可执行。
- keyed pending job merge替换实际任务的cancel token，却不更新progress authority保存的token；任务启动后按JobId取消会命中旧token，latest work可能继续运行。
- Editor job shutdown只广播合作式取消并等到deadline；超时后产品仍关闭project/settings/host state，活任务没有join、隔离或late-commit barrier。
- Job progress可合并，但Started/Completed/Failed/Cancelled进入无界lifecycle `VecDeque`；consumer暂停或长期落后时，pending admission不能阻止内存持续增长。
- Editor persistent settings在`set/clear`后先发布并hot apply，只有Scene Viewport三个Project值显式submit；User locale/design tokens/keymap/job quota可在API成功后重启丢失。
- Preferences只有无生产caller的静态modal资产，既不能枚举当前11个setting，也不消费plugin settings page；没有搜索、scope/origin、apply/reset或持久化错误流程。
- locale bundle每种语言只有54个key，而248个Editor ZUI资产仍有3,201处文本属性字面值；生产无i18n topic consumer和全shell revision rebuild，切换会形成混合语言。
- Toast按`NotificationId`字典序选择current item，但lifetime从publish开始；后来的短寿命error可在从未显示前过期，固定ID重复事件又被产品静默吞掉。
- 普通toast/progress只令Notification Center `visibility=visible`，仍保持closed/non-interactive且没有用户入口；live项消失后所谓history同步清空。
- Notification bridge的`severity=error|...|kind=toast`会被parser把后置`kind`再次当tone解析，最终将error/warning/success row全部覆盖成info。
- Editor每条日志都在全局emission mutex内同步创建/检查/打开/写入/flush项目文件，并由producer线程继续drain唯一event sink；日志storm或慢盘可直接冻结UI、job与Play控制路径。
- Editor生产没有`EditorTopic::log()` subscriber，但零subscriber dispatch仍被报告为`Delivered`；317处status写入、`tracing`、runtime diagnostics、Plugin/Import又未收敛到同一Console authority。
- Editor rolling logs没有总bytes/age/file-count配额、session/process/build identity或crash fence；I/O错误延迟到首次append且多数caller丢弃，既可无界耗盘也可静默丢失事故证据。
- Editor Move/Attach/CreateSplit布局命令先detach再验证目标，Reset布局又绕过dirty document close decision；非法drop或重置偏好可破坏布局并静默丢失未保存会话。
- Project workspace restore先清空toolkit/registry/session再逐实例恢复，page preset又先合并全部document tabs再重建有损split；任一中途失败或普通page切换都可留下半恢复或被重排的工作区。
- Layout workspace/preset没有统一bounded validator、migration、unknown-plugin placeholder和LKG；原生窗口placement也没有monitor/work-area/DPI/state或用户实际move/resize回写。
- 默认asset registry没有Animation toolkit；Sequence/Graph/State Machine虽然被索引，生产`OpenAsset`仍只显示“No toolkit”，现有打开测试靠手工注入toolkit绕过断路。
- Animation Sequence/Graph可见body仍是空slot；Scrub固定frame 0，Add Node发送不支持的`State`，多个高级animation workspace则只写“Preview/Apply queued”静态反馈。
- Animation mutation被标为transaction delegated却直接原地改session，无undo/transaction ID；同时缺共享semantic compiler和runtime一致preview，Editor可保存无效Graph/State Machine并伪装playing。
- 默认linked Editor catalog不包含Material/Particles/Shader Graph/VFX Graph，builtin Material/MaterialGraph/Shader又无toolkit；Material插件引用的graph ZUI和default template物理缺失。
- Material与Particles authoring operation只有descriptor没有factory，Particles菜单disabled且ZUI为空；Core/extension Workbench仍以固定字符串伪造compile、simulation和preview结果。
- 三套Material/Shader Graph schema互不兼容，importer只查Output、plugin compiler只折叠base color，Shader/VFX executor又是no-op；Editor没有transactional document、target artifact或runtime一致preview。
- Terrain Editor三份plugin resource物理缺失，Import/Create/Open/Sculpt operation无factory，factory-backed scene mode的open failure handoff仍未修复。
- Terrain runtime明确使用backend-not-installed诊断导入器，现有Terrain asset在graphics/scene没有生产consumer；Foliage/Scatter与World Partition runtime domain则不存在。
- Terrain/Foliage/Scatter/Level Streaming四份Workbench以固定64/96 cells、64K/84K instances、HLOD和warning伪造build/preview/load，且没有transactional world document、partition manifest或streaming authority。
- builtin registry识别Sound但不给asset toolkit，default linked Editor catalog也不装配Sound；仓内没有Audio Clip editor、waveform、import settings或安全只读preview fallback。
- Sound插件注册33个authoring operation却没有任何factory；Mixer仅三个output按钮有route，同样会命中`MissingFactory`，三类component drawer没有event。
- Sound五份surface以29个业务`Space`占位宣称Mixer、Spatial与Acoustic能力；真实live-output controller只在测试构造，callback遥测字段也从未生产更新。
- Editor没有Sound transaction/audition authority，Runtime又没有AudioSource/Listener/Volume scene system，无法证明当前source revision通过当前graph/device形成可听结果。
- builtin Physics Material没有toolkit，default linked Editor catalog也不装配Physics；四份Physics插件surface又以11个业务`Space`和0 event发布能力。
- Ragdoll“Generate From Skeleton”只打开空view，真实generator无production caller，Runtime asset pipeline也没有Ragdoll kind/import/artifact/product spawn闭环。
- Physics debug toggle只打开空view，既有overlay builder没有`ViewportOverlayProviderRegistration`或production caller，2026-08-01 failure仍为open。
- Collision Proxy与Physics Collision两份Workbench的38条route只写固定18 proxies、124 bodies、32 contacts和queued反馈，仓内没有production physics cook job/artifact链。
- Navigation两类Bake的Runtime `prepare`固定失败，而focused test仍断言相同operation成功，Editor可撤销command骨架因而无法构成产品Bake闭环。
- Navigation插件11个业务区域仍为无controller/data provider的`Space`；Bake列表、诊断、进度和两个asset toolkit也没有产品数据owner。
- Navigation overlay frame/provider虽真实存在，但toggle/filter不可执行、只读PIE mirror且逐帧复制完整snapshot；编辑态query/tile/cost调试缺失。
- Navmesh AI Workbench用固定NavMesh_Main、18 tiles、96 polys、4 agents和Door_A03反馈伪装Rebuild/Query/Commit，与插件形成双authority。
- Data Table与Save Data Workbench用固定DT_Items/128 rows/512 refs、AutoSave_01/SaveData v4/LZ4/Cloud queued伪装业务状态；route与field handler只改control字符串，没有document、job、artifact、storage或runtime receipt。
- 通用`DataAsset`没有row schema、stable row/field ID、migration、typed runtime accessor或reference manifest，builtin registry又不给Data factory/toolkit；它不能被界面改名后当作Data Table。
- Runtime没有player/profile/platform user/slot SaveGame service；DynamicScene session archive虽有真实artifact/writer/retention，但World snapshot会遗漏任意plugin/typed component，也没有SaveGame schema catalog、transactional load或cloud conflict合同。
- TOML/JSON/YAML/XML导入会同时持有完整source与canonical value且无source/depth/node/alias/CPU预算，XML递归投影还丢mixed-content顺序和namespace identity；深或恶意输入可阻塞/击穿Editor。
- Console Diagnostics、Runtime Diagnostics、Performance与Telemetry Dashboard四张Workbench用固定session/frame/actor/event/DAU/crash数据和固定成功反馈冒充产品，没有对应session、provider、query或receipt。
- builtin Runtime Diagnostics只收集Editor宿主Core与UI reflector，不请求动态子Runtime的完整diagnostics response，也不投影本地`DiagnosticStore` series；产品名掩盖了source authority差异。
- Editor与子Runtime以独立`Instant`原点记录profile，却直接偏移ID并拼接数组；capture/reset/export又是同步非事务双写，无法形成可信跨进程时间线与唯一artifact。
- 可见diagnostics pane会在presentation重算中同步采集、深复制store/rings并等待FFI；33个render stats叶文件包含648类`render.*`路径，而store总series与动态metadata没有全局cardinality预算。
- Telemetry没有event schema/provider、consent、redaction、endpoint、tenant、retention或deletion治理；`runtime_diagnostics`插件同时引用缺失资源并与builtin重复占有同一pane identity。
- Lobby Editor与Matchmaking Editor用固定`Lobby_Default`、8 slots/4 players、6 queues/128 players、latency和warning冒充产品；40个binding只切选中态、改control字符串或写固定反馈。
- 仓内没有Online Service、Identity、Lobby、Party、Matchmaking Ticket、Allocation或Backfill production domain；底层`NetManager`只有socket/HTTP/WebSocket/event/diagnostics，不能替代在线产品authority。
- Net Editor未进入默认first-party Editor catalog；即使手工注册，5个template/default document资源不存在且6个operation无factory，当前Create/Validate/Compile都会断路。
- Replication Schema只有asset/graph descriptor，没有canonical document、stable wire ID、semantic compiler、artifact、schema migration或Runtime install；Runtime manager仍是未连接World/Reflection/transport的String/raw-byte内存模型。
- Editor Play request/backend只支持一个child，没有Dedicated/Listen/Client拓扑、client count、port/account/join plan或per-link latency/loss仿真，Lobby/Matchmaking的Simulate因而不产生多人行为证据。
- Source Control Workspace固定显示`CL_2048`、18 files、2 conflicts和6 checks；仓内没有Git/libgit2/Perforce等provider、repository identity、真实file state/diff/changelist operation，却仍在冲突存在时返回Submit queued。
- Automation Report固定显示642 tests、7 failed、3 flakes及Worker_03/09/11，不消费真实authoring commandlet或Tooling10的TestPlan/Attempt/Result/Artifact/ValidationSet，Validate/Publish只写固定反馈。
- Project Overview extension固定显示NebulaGame/M3/Healthy/Win64 Success/72% coverage/7 tasks/3 jobs并允许直接编辑Health，与只投影真实project/catalog元数据的内置Project Overview形成双authority。
- Build Export与Plugin Manager extension同样通过generic preview action伪造62% cook/CDN和18 installed/3 updates；五张Production Workspace合计100个binding，没有连接各自真实owner。
- Editor没有冻结saved document generation、source change set、ValidationSet、BuildArtifactSet与policy digest的SubmissionCandidate；当前Submit/Publish无法证明结果属于待提交内容，也没有race、补偿、idempotency或immutable receipt。
- 资源“stable UUID”由未承诺跨版本稳定的Rust `DefaultHasher`生成，并进入生产资产/资源引用；工具链或平台变化可能把同一标签/locator映射为新身份，当前没有算法版本、迁移或redirect catalog。
- canonical text为对象每个value持续持有一个临时文件，文档byte cap不限制entry/open handle/spool；world query又会在producer端完整扫描、反射并序列化全部实体，两条路径都可在consumer限制生效前耗尽进程资源。
- `ResourceLocator`依赖宿主OS `Path::components`且参与stable ID，同一字符串可跨Windows/Linux规范化分裂；`RelPath`对root containment的承诺也只覆盖词法，不覆盖symlink/junction/reparse point。

[zircon_runtime/72-runtime-core-lifecycle-registry-concurrency-shutdown-current-source-review.md](zircon_runtime/72-runtime-core-lifecycle-registry-concurrency-shutdown-current-source-review.md)对Runtime01的当前源码做增量深审：graph/service拓扑、依赖闭包、veto原子性、generation call guard和dynamic session shutdown已是可保留底座，但全局Condvar会重复登记waiter，single activation closure可与provider unload竞态，service对象在registry锁内析构，batch partial token取得失败会泄漏transition，static App/Editor没有owned close，声明图shutdown又会被未启动module中止。新增6项P0、18项P1、8项P2及40项资格门；Runtime46 lazy factory panic与Runtime50裸Arc路径保持原owner，Runtime01两个failure无新Cargo证据并继续open。

[zircon_runtime/73-runtime-ui-style-theme-token-cascade-selector-pseudo-state-invalidation-transition-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/73-runtime-ui-style-theme-token-cascade-selector-pseudo-state-invalidation-transition-product-integration-review.md)对Runtime UI style/theme/cascade做当前源码增量深审：tuple specificity、V2 static/runtime rule分离、token provenance与局部pseudo-state重算可保留，但默认Closed component scope、`:part()`/`:host`不可执行，gameplay不消费style import/component-local style且没有theme owner，theme hot reload只标dirty不重算，五个transition component没有runner。新增5项P0、48项P1、12项P2及44项资格门；Runtime11A继续拥有tree/layout/input/a11y与总体reload lifecycle，Runtime43/64、Editor01/23和Tooling32保持父owner。

[zircon_runtime/75-runtime-ui-component-catalog-widget-behavior-state-reducer-interaction-semantics-accessibility-product-integration-review.md](zircon_runtime/75-runtime-ui-component-catalog-widget-behavior-state-reducer-interaction-semantics-accessibility-product-integration-review.md)对Runtime UI component catalog与live widget行为做当前源码增量深审：descriptor、reducers、surface interaction与AccessKit底座可保留，但showcase/Material/Editor-local多authority分裂，v2不执行descriptor admission，catalog reducer与live surface断开，输入、behavior、render、dirty与a11y依赖互相矛盾的字符串classifier，live mutation又允许schema外写入。新增6项P0、48项P1、12项P2及48项资格门；Runtime11A/73/74与Editor01/23保留tree、style、binding/reload与authoring父边界。
